### FILE: backend/README.md  (50 lines)
```
# PCOS-XAI Backend

FastAPI + MongoDB backend for a multimodal PCOS research screening application.

## Included
- Patient and Doctor accounts
- Password hashing
- OTP at registration and login
- JWT access/refresh tokens
- Patient-to-doctor temporary access code
- Clinical model adapter
- EfficientNet-B0 gated-attention ultrasound classifier
- Equal-weight fusion of available model probabilities
- Clinical analysis result adapter
- SHAP adapter
- LLM report adapter
- PDF generation
- Report history
- OTP-protected report downloads

## Important
The API accepts clinical values and optional ultrasound images. The image classifier checkpoint is under `backend/pcos_efficientnet_b0.pt/pcos_efficientnet_b0/`. Set `ULTRASOUND_PCOS_CLASS_INDEX` to the output index used for the PCOS class (defaults to `1`; confirm against training labels). The chec ...[line cut]

## Run
1. Python 3.11+
2. MongoDB running
3. Copy `.env.example` to `.env` and set JWT_SECRET
4. `pip install -r requirements.txt`
5. `uvicorn app.main:app --reload`
6. Open `http://127.0.0.1:8000/docs`

## Core APIs
POST /api/auth/register
POST /api/auth/verify-registration
POST /api/auth/login
POST /api/auth/verify-login
POST /api/auth/refresh
GET /api/users/me
POST /api/users/patient-access-code
POST /api/users/doctor-access/{code}
POST /api/analysis/run
GET /api/analysis/history
POST /api/reports/{analysis_id}/generate
GET /api/reports
POST /api/reports/{report_id}/download/request
POST /api/reports/{report_id}/download/verify
GET /api/health

## Production hardening
Use a real SMS/email OTP provider, HTTPS, rate limiting, secret management, audit logs, stronger doctor-patient authorization, and formal clinical validation before real clinical use.
```

### FILE: frontend/README.md  (114 lines)
```
# PCOS Screen

A React frontend for a multimodal PCOS screening research project. An ultrasound image goes to an EfficientNet-B0 gated-attention model, clinical values go to a Random Forest, and the backend averages the available probabilities.

It is built for the FastAPI backend described in the Backend Spec: OTP sign-in, patient and doctor roles, saved screenings, and PDF reports.

## Run it

Start the backend first (`uvicorn app.main:app --reload` in `backend/`, which serves `http://127.0.0.1:8000`), then:

```bash
npm install
npm run dev        # http://localhost:5173
npm run build      # production build in dist/
```

Needs Node 20.19 or newer. The backend already allows `http://localhost:5173` through CORS. To point at another address, copy `.env.example` to `.env.local` and set `VITE_API_URL`. For a deployed site, add the site's address to the backend's CORS origins.

## Pages

| Page | Address | Who | What it does |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Home | `#/` | everyone | Overview with the photo banner and a preview of the outputs |
| Screen | `#/screen` | signed in | Upload a scan, enter clinical values, run the analysis, make a PDF |
| My reports / Patients | `#/reports` | signed in | Patients: their screenings, PDF reports, a doctor access code. Doctors: enter a patient's code, browse saved results |
| Result | `#/reports/<id>` | signed in | One saved result with its PDF reports |
| Sign in | `#/sign-in` | everyone | Sign in or create an account, both confirmed by an emailed code |
| About PCOS | `#/about` | everyone | What PCOS is and how it is diagnosed, with sources |
| How it works | `#/how-it-works` | everyone | The pipeline, an example result, and the limits |

It uses a small hash router, so the built site works from any static host without server setup.

## How it talks to the backend

Everything goes through `src/lib/api.js`. Sessions are kept in `localStorage` (`pcos.session`); an expired access token is renewed once with the refresh token before the user is signed out.

| Action | Call |
| --- | --- |
| Create account | `POST /api/auth/register`, then `POST /api/auth/verify-registration` |
| Sign in | `POST /api/auth/login`, then `POST /api/auth/verify-login` (an unverified account is sent to registration verification instead) |
| Run a screening | `POST /api/analysis/run`, multipart: `clinical_json` (JSON text) and optional `ultrasound` image file |
| History | `GET /api/analysis/history` (patients), `GET /api/analysis/doctor-history` (doctors), `GET /api/analysis/{id}` |
| PDF report | `POST /api/reports/{analysis_id}/generate`, `GET /api/reports`, `POST /api/reports/{id}/download/request`, then `POST /api/reports/{id}/download/verify?otp=...` |
| Doctor sharing | `POST /api/users/patient-access-code` (patient), `POST /api/users/doctor-access/{code}` (doctor) |

Errors use FastAPI's `{ "detail": ... }` and are shown as written. If the server cannot be reached, the message names the address and mentions CORS.

### The clinical form

`src/lib/features.js` lists the 38 inputs the form asks for, with the API key and model column name for each. BMI, FSH/LH and waist:hip ratio are not asked for, because the server works them out. Blank fields are left out of `clinical_json`, so the server's defaults apply. The sanity ranges (`min`/` ...[line cut]

Yes/No answers are sent as `true`/`false`. If the backend answers `422 Invalid clinical data` for them, set `YES_NO_FORMAT = 'string'` in `src/config.js`.

### The results

`src/lib/results.js` reads `clinical`, `ultrasound`, `combined` and `explainability` from the response. The screens show only what the backend returns:

- each available model probability and the combined model label (PCOS at 50% or above), with a band for results near the threshold;
- the Random Forest's overall feature importances next to the values the model was given, marked "default" where the server filled a value in;
- any plain text the backend puts directly under `explainability` (for example from `build_explanation()`).

There is no heatmap or weight slider; the backend fixes multimodal weights at 50/50.

## Things to confirm

- **Blood group codes** (`BLOOD_GROUPS` in `features.js`) follow the public Kaggle PCOS dataset (11 = A+ ... 18 = AB−). Check them against your training data.
- **Cycle length** is labelled with a note that the training data looks like days of bleeding (mostly 2 to 12). Confirm and rename the label if needed.
- **The 35% and 65% band edges** in `config.js` are a display aid, not a clinical rule.
- `MIN_CLINICAL_FIELDS` (8) only decides when the result page warns that the clinical numbers mostly reflect defaults.

## Notes on the backend

Current behavior and remaining considerations:

- The API returns `NOT_AVAILABLE` for the clinical modality unless all 38 form values are present (with BMI and two ratios computed from source values). Without an image, ultrasound is `NOT_AVAILABLE`. Fusion uses only modalities that produced a probability.
- `GET /api/analysis/doctor-history` returns every patient's analyses to any doctor, and anyone can register with the role `doctor`. The access code does not limit what a doctor sees.
- The active analysis route returns a Grad-CAM++ heatmap overlay for ultrasound images; the older `ultrasound_model.explain()` helper is not used by that route.
- Uploaded images and PDFs are stored on the server's disk. They contain health data, so consider access controls and retention.

## Pictures

Each page has its own photo in `public/images/`:

| Page | File |
| --- | --- |
| Home | `consultation.jpg` |
| Screen | `screen.jpg` |
| About PCOS | `about.jpg` |
| How it works | `how.jpg` |
| Sign in | `signin.jpg` |

The crop and zoom of each one is set in `src/content/images.js`. To use another photo, replace the file (3:2 landscape, 1920 px wide or more, subject on the right with calm space on the left, no readable text or logos) and adjust its crop there. Only use images you have the right to publish.

The photographs are AI-generated, and the footer says so. Some carry a small generator mark in a corner. It is left as it came, and some pages crop it out as part of the layout. If you replace the photos, keep the footer note accurate.

The small ultrasound pictures on Home, About and How it works are synthetic images drawn in the browser (`src/lib/synthetic.js`), and the page says so. Replace them by setting `src` for `example`, `typical` or `pcom` in `src/content/images.js`. Clinical scans need consent or proper de-identification ...[line cut]

## Project layout

```
src/
  config.js            names, API address, upload limits, bands
  lib/                 api.js, auth.jsx, features.js, results.js, router.jsx, format.js, synthetic.js
  pages/               Home, Screen, Reports, ReportDetail, SignIn, About, How
  components/          Header, PageHero, ClinicalForm, ReportView, ReportActions, ReportDownload, ...
  content/             images.js, sample.js (fixed example output for Home and How it works)
public/images/         consultation.jpg
```

## Accessibility

Skip link, visible focus, labelled fields, errors announced with `role="alert"`, results in a live region, colour never the only signal (band names are written out), and reduced-motion support.

This is a research prototype. The outputs are model estimates and not a diagnosis.
```

```
# Backend Spec

Scope: this document records the backend state and local HTTP smoke tests from 2026-10-11. The API examples below are actual local responses where possible. Generated IDs are test values; bearer tokens and OTPs are redacted. Training notebook outputs are not proof that a currently loaded artifact ha ...[line cut]

## Current Implementation Note (2026-10-12)

The active workflow accepts clinical JSON and an optional ultrasound image. The image checkpoint is a PyTorch state dict extracted into `backend/pcos_efficientnet_b0.pt/pcos_efficientnet_b0/`; the backend reconstructs an EfficientNet-B0 feature extractor with gated attention and a two-class head. Se ...[line cut]

## 1. HOW TO RUN

- **Framework:** FastAPI (`FastAPI(title=settings.APP_NAME, version="1.0.0")`).
- **Start command:** from `backend/`: `uvicorn app.main:app --reload`. The README's documented API URL is `http://127.0.0.1:8000/docs`.
- **Default host / port:** Uvicorn defaults to `127.0.0.1:8000` when no host/port flags are supplied. The local smoke test used `127.0.0.1:8000`.
- **Python:** backend README says Python 3.11+. Local runtime used for this document: Python 3.13.7.
- **MongoDB:** default URI is `mongodb://localhost:27017`, database `pcos_xai`. On connection failure, startup logs a notice and switches to an in-memory mock database; data in that fallback is not persistent.
- **CORS:** enabled with credentials, all methods and headers. Explicit origins include `http://localhost:5173` and `http://127.0.0.1:5173` (among several other local ports). The origin regex also allows any `http` or `https` port on `localhost` or `127.0.0.1`.
- **Authentication:** no API key. Protected routes require `Authorization: Bearer <access_token>` using a JWT issued by the auth endpoints. Login and registration use emailed OTP verification. `/api/analysis/run` requires a valid access token despite `get_optional_user`'s name; `/api/analysis/histor ...[line cut]
- **Configuration:** settings can be supplied through environment variables or `backend/.env`; see `backend/app/core/config.py`. The source has a development JWT-secret default; set a strong `JWT_SECRET` outside development. OTP lifetime defaults to 5 minutes, maximum failed attempts to 5, access to ...[line cut]
- **Docs:** FastAPI interactive docs are at `/docs`; OpenAPI JSON is at `/openapi.json`.

## 2. ENDPOINTS

Every route below is mounted under the prefix shown. Each code block is copied verbatim from its route function in the active backend.

### `GET /`
Returns service name, running status, and docs path.

```python
@app.get("/")
async def root(): return {"service":settings.APP_NAME,"status":"running","docs":"/docs"}
```

### `GET /api/health`
Pings MongoDB and returns health status.

```python
@router.get("/health")
async def health(): await get_db().command("ping"); return {"status":"healthy","database":"connected"}
```

### `POST /api/auth/register`
Creates a user and starts registration OTP verification.

```python
@router.post("/register")
async def register(data: RegisterRequest, bg: BackgroundTasks):
    db = get_db()
    email = data.email.lower().strip()
    
    existing_user = await db.users.find_one({"email": email})
    if existing_user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Email is already registered")

    phone = data.phone.strip() if data.phone and data.phone.strip() else None
    if phone:
        existing_phone = await db.users.find_one({"phone": phone})
        if existing_phone:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Phone number is already registered")

    uid = str(uuid4())
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    
    user_doc = {
        "_id": uid,
        "email": email,
        "password_hash": hash_password(data.password),
        "full_name": data.full_name.strip(),
        "role": data.role,
        "verified": False,
        "created_at": now,
        "updated_at": now
    }
    if phone:
        user_doc["phone"] = phone
    
    try:
        await db.users.insert_one(user_doc)
    except DuplicateKeyError as e:
        if "phone" in str(e):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Phone number is already registered")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Email is already registered")
    
    otp = await create_otp_challenge(uid, "registration")
    bg.add_task(send_otp_email, email, otp, "registration")
    
    return {
        "message": "Registration created successfully. Please verify the OTP sent to your email to continue.",
        "user_id": uid
    }
```

### `POST /api/auth/verify-registration`
Validates the registration OTP, marks the account verified, and issues access and refresh tokens.

```python
@router.post("/verify-registration")
async def verify_registration(data: VerifyOTPRequest):
    db = get_db()
    
    is_valid = await verify_otp_challenge(data.user_id, "registration", data.otp)
    if not is_valid:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid or expired OTP code")
        
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    r = await db.users.update_one(
        {"_id": data.user_id},
        {"$set": {"verified": True, "updated_at": now}}
    )
    
    u = await db.users.find_one({"_id": data.user_id})
    if not u:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User account not found")
        
    access_token = create_access_token(u["_id"], u["role"])
    refresh_token = create_refresh_token(u["_id"], u["role"])
    
    return {
        "message": "Account verified successfully",
        "access_token": ***REDACTED***,
        "refresh_token": ***REDACTED***,
        "token_type": ***REDACTED***,
        "role": u["role"],
        "user_id": u["_id"],
        "full_name": u.get("full_name", "")
    }
```

### `POST /api/auth/login`
Checks email/password. Sends a registration OTP for unverified accounts or a login OTP for verified accounts.

```python
@router.post("/login")
async def login(data: LoginRequest, bg: BackgroundTasks):
    db = get_db()
    email = data.email.lower().strip()
    
    u = await db.users.find_one({"email": email})
    if not u or not verify_password(data.password, u["password_hash"]):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid email address or password")
        
    user_role = u.get("role", "patient")

    # Check if account is verified
    if not u.get("verified", False):
        otp = await create_otp_challenge(u["_id"], "registration")
        bg.add_task(send_otp_email, u["email"], otp, "registration")
        return {
            "message": "Account is not verified yet. Verification OTP code sent to your email.",
            "user_id": u["_id"],
            "requires_verification": True,
            "role": user_role
        }
        
    otp = await create_otp_challenge(u["_id"], "login")
    bg.add_task(send_otp_email, u["email"], otp, "login")
    
    return {
        "message": "Verification code sent to your email.",
        "user_id": u["_id"],
        "role": user_role
    }
```

### `POST /api/auth/verify-login`
Validates the login OTP and issues access and refresh tokens.

```python
@router.post("/verify-login")
async def verify_login(data: VerifyLoginOTPRequest):
    db = get_db()
    
    is_valid = await verify_otp_challenge(data.user_id, "login", data.otp)
    if not is_valid:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid or expired OTP code")
        
    u = await db.users.find_one({"_id": data.user_id})
    if not u:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User account not found")
        
    access_token = create_access_token(u["_id"], u["role"])
    refresh_token = create_refresh_token(u["_id"], u["role"])
    
    return {
        "access_token": ***REDACTED***,
        "refresh_token": ***REDACTED***,
        "token_type": ***REDACTED***,
        "role": u["role"],
        "user_id": u["_id"],
        "full_name": u.get("full_name", "")
    }
```

### `POST /api/auth/refresh`
Validates a refresh token and returns a new access token.

```python
@router.post("/refresh")
async def refresh(data: RefreshRequest):
    p = decode_token(data.refresh_token)
    if not p or p.get("type") != "refresh":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid refresh token")
        
    u = await get_db().users.find_one({"_id": p["sub"]})
    if not u:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="User account not found")
        
    return {
        "access_token": create_access_token(u["_id"], u["role"]),
        "token_type": ***REDACTED***
    }
```

### `GET /api/users/me`
Returns selected fields for the authenticated user.

```python
@router.get("/me")
async def me(user=Depends(get_current_user)):
    return {
        key: user.get(key)
        for key in [
            "user_id",
            "email",
            "phone",
            "full_name",
            "role",
            "verified"
        ]
    }
```

### `POST /api/users/patient-access-code`
Creates a patient access code that expires after 30 minutes.

```python
@router.post("/patient-access-code")
async def create_code(
    user=Depends(require_roles("patient"))
):
    code = f"PCOS-{str(uuid4())[:8].upper()}"

    expires_at = (
        datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        + timedelta(minutes=30)
    )

    await get_db().patient_access.insert_one({
        "code": code,
        "patient_id": user["user_id"],
        "expires_at": expires_at,
        "used": False
    })

    return {
        "access_code": code,
        "expires_at": expires_at
    }
```

### `POST /api/users/doctor-access/{code}`
Consumes a valid patient access code for an authenticated doctor.

```python
@router.post("/doctor-access/{code}")
async def doctor_access(
    code: str,
    doctor=Depends(require_roles("doctor"))
):
    db = get_db()

    access_record = await db.patient_access.find_one({
        "code": code,
        "used": False
    })

    if not access_record:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Invalid or expired access code"
        )

    expires_at = access_record["expires_at"]

    # MongoDB may return a timezone-naive datetime.
    # Treat it as UTC before comparing.
    if expires_at.tzinfo is None:
        expires_at = expires_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    else:
        expires_at = expires_at.astimezone(timezone.utc)

    if expires_at <= datetime.now(timezone.utc):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Invalid or expired access code"
        )

    await db.patient_access.update_one(
        {
            "_id": access_record["_id"]
        },
        {
            "$set": {
                "used": True,
                "doctor_id": doctor["user_id"],
                "authorized_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            }
        }
    )

    return {
        "patient_id": access_record["patient_id"],
        "message": "Patient access granted"
    }
```

### `POST /api/analysis/run`
Validates submitted clinical JSON, optionally processes an ultrasound image, runs available modality models and fusion, and saves an analysis if database insertion works.

```python
@router.post("/run")
async def run_analysis(
    request: Request,
    clinical_json: str = Form(...),
    ultrasound: UploadFile | None = File(None)
):
    user = await get_optional_user(request)
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(401, "Sign in before generating a report")
    patient_id = user["user_id"]
    patient_name = user.get("full_name", "Patient")

    # Parse clinical JSON
    try:
        raw_clinical = json.loads(clinical_json)
        clinical = ClinicalInput.model_validate(raw_clinical)
        clinical_dict = clinical.model_dump()
        # Merge raw dict keys for extra fields like maritalStatus, irregularPeriods, etc.
        clinical_dict.update(raw_clinical)
    except Exception as e:
        raise HTTPException(422, f"Invalid clinical data: {e}")

    # Process ultrasound file if uploaded
    image_path = None
    if ultrasound and ultrasound.filename:
        suffix = Path(ultrasound.filename).suffix.lower()
        if suffix not in {".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp"}:
            raise HTTPException(400, "Unsupported image format")
        data = await ultrasound.read()
        if len(data) > settings.MAX_UPLOAD_MB * 1024 * 1024:
            raise HTTPException(413, "File too large")
        
        upload_dir = Path(settings.UPLOAD_DIR)
        upload_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        image_path = str(upload_dir / f"{uuid4()}{suffix}")
        Path(image_path).write_bytes(data)

    # ML Predictions
    cr = clinical_model.predict(clinical_dict)

    ur = ultrasound_model.predict(image_path) if image_path else {
        "prediction": "NOT_AVAILABLE",
        "probability": None,
        "model_version": ultrasound_model.model_version
    }
    combined = fusion_engine.combine(cr, ur)
    explanation = build_explanation(
        clinical_model, clinical_dict, ultrasound_model, image_path
    )
    aid = str(uuid4())
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    # Save to MongoDB if available
    try:
        db = get_db()
        await db.analyses.insert_one({
            "_id": aid,
            "patient_id": patient_id,
            "patient_name": patient_name,
            "performed_by": patient_id,
            "clinical_input": clinical_dict,
            "ultrasound_image": image_path,
            "clinical": cr,
            "ultrasound": ur,
            "combined": combined,
            "explainability": explanation,
            "created_at": now
        })
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[Analysis] DB insert notice (continuing without DB): {e}")

    return {
        "analysis_id": aid,
        "patient_id": patient_id,
        "patient_name": patient_name,
        "clinical_input": clinical_dict,
        "clinical": cr,
        "ultrasound": ur,
        "clinical_report": cr,
        "ultrasound_report": ur,
        "combined": combined,
        "explainability": explanation,
        "created_at": now.isoformat()
    }
```

### `GET /api/analysis/history`
Returns the current patient's analyses, newest first; without a valid user it returns `[]`.

```python
@router.get("/history")
async def history(request: Request):
    user = await get_optional_user(request)
    if not user:
        return []
    try:
        db = get_db()
        cur = db.analyses.find({"patient_id": user["user_id"]}).sort("created_at", -1)
        out = []
        async for x in cur:
            out.append({
                "analysis_id": x["_id"],
                "patient_name": x.get("patient_name", "Patient"),
                "patient_id": x.get("patient_id"),
                "clinical": x.get("clinical", {}),
                "ultrasound": x.get("ultrasound", {}),
                "combined": x.get("combined", {}),
                "created_at": x["created_at"].isoformat() if isinstance(x["created_at"], datetime) else str(x["created_at"])
            })
        return out
    except Exception as error:
        print(f"[Analysis] History read failed: {error}")
        raise HTTPException(500, "Unable to load saved reports")
```

### `GET /api/analysis/doctor-history`
Requires doctor role and returns all saved analyses with patient names where available.

```python
@router.get("/doctor-history")
async def doctor_history(request: Request):
    user = await get_optional_user(request)
    if not user or user.get("role") != "doctor":
        raise HTTPException(403, "Doctor access required")

    out = []
    async for analysis in get_db().analyses.find({}).sort("created_at", -1):
        patient = await get_db().users.find_one({"_id": analysis.get("patient_id")})
        out.append({
            "analysis_id": analysis["_id"],
            "patient_name": (patient or {}).get("full_name", analysis.get("patient_name", "Patient")),
            "patient_id": analysis.get("patient_id"),
            "created_at": analysis["created_at"].isoformat() if isinstance(analysis.get("created_at"), datetime) else str(analysis.get("created_at")),
            "clinical": analysis.get("clinical", {}),
            "ultrasound": analysis.get("ultrasound", {}),
        })
    return out
```

### `GET /api/analysis/{analysis_id}`
Returns one saved report; doctors can query any saved analysis, while other users are restricted to their own patient ID.

async def get_analysis(analysis_id: str, request: Request):
    user = await get_optional_user(request)
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(401, "Authentication required")

    query = {"_id": analysis_id}
    if user.get("role") != "doctor":
        query["patient_id"] = user["user_id"]

        raise HTTPException(404, "Saved report not found")

    return {
        "analysis_id": analysis["_id"],
        "patient_id": analysis["patient_id"],
        "patient_name": analysis.get("patient_name", "Patient"),

        "ultrasound_report": analysis.get("ultrasound", {}),

        "explainability": analysis.get("explainability", {}),
        "created_at": analysis["created_at"].isoformat() if isinstance(analysis["created_at"], datetime) else str(analysis["created_at"]),
    }

import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from uuid import uuid4

from fastapi import APIRouter, File, Form, HTTPException, Request, UploadFile

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.security import decode_token
from app.db.mongo import get_db
from app.explainability.service import build_explanation
from app.ml.clinical_model import clinical_model
from app.ml.fusion_engine import fusion_engine
from app.ml.ultrasound_model import ultrasound_model
from app.schemas.analysis import ClinicalInput

router = APIRouter()


async def get_optional_user(request: Request):
    auth_header = request.headers.get("Authorization")
    if not auth_header or not auth_header.startswith("Bearer "):
        return None
    token = auth_header.split(" ")[1]
    payload = decode_token(token)
    if not payload or payload.get("type") != "access":
        return None
    try:
        user = await get_db().users.find_one({"_id": payload["sub"]})
        if user:
            user["user_id"] = user["_id"]
            return user
    except Exception:
        pass
    return {"user_id": payload.get("sub", "guest_user"), "role": "patient"}


@router.post("/run")
async def run_analysis(
    request: Request,
    clinical_json: str = Form(...),
    ultrasound: UploadFile | None = File(None),
):
    user = await get_optional_user(request)
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(401, "Sign in before generating a report")
    patient_id = user["user_id"]
    patient_name = user.get("full_name", "Patient")

    try:
        raw_clinical = json.loads(clinical_json)
        clinical = ClinicalInput.model_validate(raw_clinical)
        clinical_dict = clinical.model_dump()
        clinical_dict.update(raw_clinical)
    except Exception as error:
        raise HTTPException(422, f"Invalid clinical data: {error}")

    image_path = None
    if ultrasound and ultrasound.filename:
        suffix = Path(ultrasound.filename).suffix.lower()
        if suffix not in {".jpg", ".jpeg", ".png", ".webp"}:
            raise HTTPException(400, "Unsupported image format")
        image_data = await ultrasound.read(settings.MAX_UPLOAD_MB * 1024 * 1024 + 1)
        if len(image_data) > settings.MAX_UPLOAD_MB * 1024 * 1024:
            raise HTTPException(413, "File too large")
        upload_dir = Path(settings.UPLOAD_DIR)
        upload_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        image_path = str(upload_dir / f"{uuid4()}{suffix}")
        Path(image_path).write_bytes(image_data)

    clinical_result = clinical_model.predict(clinical_dict)
    ultrasound_result = ultrasound_model.predict(image_path) if image_path else {
        "prediction": "NOT_AVAILABLE",
        "probability": None,
        "model_version": ultrasound_model.model_version,
        "message": (
            "Ultrasound estimate unavailable because no image was "
            "provided."
        ),
    }
    combined = fusion_engine.combine(clinical_result, ultrasound_result)
    explanation = build_explanation(
        clinical_model,
        clinical_dict,
        ultrasound_model,
        image_path,
        clinical_result=clinical_result,
    )
    analysis_id = str(uuid4())
    created_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    try:
        await get_db().analyses.insert_one({
            "_id": analysis_id,
            "patient_id": patient_id,
            "patient_name": patient_name,
            "performed_by": patient_id,
            "clinical_input": clinical_dict,
            "ultrasound_image": image_path,
            "clinical": clinical_result,
            "ultrasound": ultrasound_result,
            "combined": combined,
            "explainability": explanation,
            "created_at": created_at,
        })
    except Exception as error:
        print(f"[Analysis] DB insert notice (continuing without DB): {error}")

    return {
        "analysis_id": analysis_id,
        "patient_id": patient_id,
        "patient_name": patient_name,
        "clinical_input": clinical_dict,
        "clinical": clinical_result,
        "ultrasound": ultrasound_result,
        "clinical_report": clinical_result,
        "ultrasound_report": ultrasound_result,
        "combined": combined,
        "explainability": explanation,
        "created_at": created_at.isoformat(),
    }


@router.get("/history")
async def history(request: Request):
    user = await get_optional_user(request)
    if not user:
        return []
    try:
        cursor = get_db().analyses.find({"patient_id": user["user_id"]}).sort("created_at", -1)
        results = []
        async for analysis in cursor:
            results.append({
                "analysis_id": analysis["_id"],
                "patient_name": analysis.get("patient_name", "Patient"),
                "patient_id": analysis.get("patient_id"),
                "clinical": analysis.get("clinical", {}),
                "ultrasound": analysis.get("ultrasound", {}),
                "combined": analysis.get("combined", {}),
                "created_at": analysis["created_at"].isoformat()
                if isinstance(analysis["created_at"], datetime)
                else str(analysis["created_at"]),
            })
        return results
    except Exception as error:
        print(f"[Analysis] History read failed: {error}")
        raise HTTPException(500, "Unable to load saved reports")


@router.get("/doctor-history")
async def doctor_history(request: Request):
    user = await get_optional_user(request)
    if not user or user.get("role") != "doctor":
        raise HTTPException(403, "Doctor access required")

    results = []
    async for analysis in get_db().analyses.find({}).sort("created_at", -1):
        patient = await get_db().users.find_one({"_id": analysis.get("patient_id")})
        results.append({
            "analysis_id": analysis["_id"],
            "patient_name": (patient or {}).get("full_name", analysis.get("patient_name", "Patient")),
            "patient_id": analysis.get("patient_id"),
            "created_at": analysis["created_at"].isoformat()
            if isinstance(analysis.get("created_at"), datetime)
            else str(analysis.get("created_at")),
            "clinical": analysis.get("clinical", {}),
            "ultrasound": analysis.get("ultrasound", {}),
        })
    return results


@router.get("/{analysis_id}")
async def get_analysis(analysis_id: str, request: Request):
    user = await get_optional_user(request)
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(401, "Authentication required")

    query = {"_id": analysis_id}
    if user.get("role") != "doctor":
        query["patient_id"] = user["user_id"]
    analysis = await get_db().analyses.find_one(query)
    if not analysis:
        raise HTTPException(404, "Saved report not found")

    return {
        "analysis_id": analysis["_id"],
        "patient_id": analysis["patient_id"],
        "patient_name": analysis.get("patient_name", "Patient"),
        "clinical_input": analysis.get("clinical_input", {}),
        "clinical": analysis.get("clinical", {}),
        "ultrasound": analysis.get("ultrasound", {}),
        "clinical_report": analysis.get("clinical", {}),
        "ultrasound_report": analysis.get("ultrasound", {}),
        "combined": analysis.get("combined", {}),
        "explainability": analysis.get("explainability", {}),
        "created_at": analysis["created_at"].isoformat()
        if isinstance(analysis["created_at"], datetime)
        else str(analysis["created_at"]),
    }

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from uuid import uuid4
from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, BackgroundTasks
from pymongo.errors import DuplicateKeyError
from app.db.mongo import get_db
from app.schemas.auth import *
from app.core.security import *
from app.core.config import settings
from app.services.otp_service import create_otp_challenge, verify_otp_challenge
from app.services.notification_service import send_otp_email

router = APIRouter()

@router.post("/register")
async def register(data: RegisterRequest, bg: BackgroundTasks):
    db = get_db()
    email = data.email.lower().strip()
    
    existing_user = await db.users.find_one({"email": email})
    if existing_user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Email is already registered")

    phone = data.phone.strip() if data.phone and data.phone.strip() else None
    if phone:
        existing_phone = await db.users.find_one({"phone": phone})
        if existing_phone:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Phone number is already registered")

    uid = str(uuid4())
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    
    user_doc = {
        "_id": uid,
        "email": email,
        "password_hash": hash_password(data.password),
        "full_name": data.full_name.strip(),
        "role": data.role,
        "verified": False,
        "created_at": now,
        "updated_at": now
    }
    if phone:
        user_doc["phone"] = phone
    
    try:
        await db.users.insert_one(user_doc)
    except DuplicateKeyError as e:
        if "phone" in str(e):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Phone number is already registered")
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail="Email is already registered")
    
    otp = await create_otp_challenge(uid, "registration")
    bg.add_task(send_otp_email, email, otp, "registration")
    
    return {
        "message": "Registration created successfully. Please verify the OTP sent to your email to continue.",
        "user_id": uid
    }


@router.post("/verify-registration")
async def verify_registration(data: VerifyOTPRequest):
    db = get_db()
    
    is_valid = await verify_otp_challenge(data.user_id, "registration", data.otp)
    if not is_valid:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid or expired OTP code")
        
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    r = await db.users.update_one(
        {"_id": data.user_id},
        {"$set": {"verified": True, "updated_at": now}}
    )
    
    u = await db.users.find_one({"_id": data.user_id})
    if not u:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User account not found")
        
    access_token = create_access_token(u["_id"], u["role"])
    refresh_token = create_refresh_token(u["_id"], u["role"])
    
    return {
        "message": "Account verified successfully",
        "access_token": ***REDACTED***,
        "refresh_token": ***REDACTED***,
        "token_type": ***REDACTED***,
        "role": u["role"],
        "user_id": u["_id"],
        "full_name": u.get("full_name", "")
    }


@router.post("/login")
async def login(data: LoginRequest, bg: BackgroundTasks):
    db = get_db()
    email = data.email.lower().strip()
    
    u = await db.users.find_one({"email": email})
    if not u or not verify_password(data.password, u["password_hash"]):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid email address or password")
        
    user_role = u.get("role", "patient")

    # Check if account is verified
    if not u.get("verified", False):
        otp = await create_otp_challenge(u["_id"], "registration")
        bg.add_task(send_otp_email, u["email"], otp, "registration")
        return {
            "message": "Account is not verified yet. Verification OTP code sent to your email.",
            "user_id": u["_id"],
            "requires_verification": True,
            "role": user_role
        }
        
    otp = await create_otp_challenge(u["_id"], "login")
    bg.add_task(send_otp_email, u["email"], otp, "login")
    
    return {
        "message": "Verification code sent to your email.",
        "user_id": u["_id"],
        "role": user_role
    }


@router.post("/verify-login")
async def verify_login(data: VerifyLoginOTPRequest):
    db = get_db()
    
    is_valid = await verify_otp_challenge(data.user_id, "login", data.otp)
    if not is_valid:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid or expired OTP code")
        
    u = await db.users.find_one({"_id": data.user_id})
    if not u:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User account not found")
        
    access_token = create_access_token(u["_id"], u["role"])
    refresh_token = create_refresh_token(u["_id"], u["role"])
    
    return {
        "access_token": ***REDACTED***,
        "refresh_token": ***REDACTED***,
        "token_type": ***REDACTED***,
        "role": u["role"],
        "user_id": u["_id"],
        "full_name": u.get("full_name", "")
    }


@router.post("/refresh")
async def refresh(data: RefreshRequest):
    p = decode_token(data.refresh_token)
    if not p or p.get("type") != "refresh":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="Invalid refresh token")
        
    u = await get_db().users.find_one({"_id": p["sub"]})
    if not u:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail="User account not found")
        
    return {
        "access_token": create_access_token(u["_id"], u["role"]),
        "token_type": ***REDACTED***
    }

from datetime import datetime,timezone
from uuid import uuid4
from fastapi import APIRouter,Depends,HTTPException,BackgroundTasks
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse
from app.core.deps import get_current_user
from app.db.mongo import get_db
from app.services.otp_service import create_otp_challenge,verify_otp_challenge
from app.services.notification_service import send_otp_email
from app.reporting.llm_service import generate_clinical_summary
from app.reporting.pdf_generator import generate_pdf
from app.core.config import settings
router=APIRouter()
@router.post("/{analysis_id}/generate")
async def generate_report(analysis_id:str,user=Depends(get_current_user)):
    db=get_db(); query={"_id":analysis_id}
    if user["role"] != "doctor": query["patient_id"] = user["user_id"]
    a=await db.analyses.find_one(query)
    if not a: raise HTTPException(404,"Analysis not found")
    rid=str(uuid4()); path=f"{settings.REPORT_DIR}/{rid}.pdf"; analysis={k:a.get(k, {}) for k in ["clinical","ultrasound","combined","explainability"]}; summary=generate_clinical_summary(analysis)
    patient=await db.users.find_one({"_id":a["patient_id"]}) or {}
    generate_pdf(path,{"user_id":a["patient_id"],"full_name":patient.get("full_name",a.get("patient_name","Patient")),"email":patient.get("email","")},analysis,summary); now=datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    await db.reports.insert_one({"_id":rid,"analysis_id":analysis_id,"patient_id":a["patient_id"],"file_path":path,"created_at":now,"status":"ready"})
    return {"report_id":rid,"analysis_id":analysis_id,"status":"ready","created_at":now.isoformat()}
@router.get("")
async def report_history(user=Depends(get_current_user)):
    query={} if user["role"] == "doctor" else {"patient_id":user["user_id"]}
    cur=get_db().reports.find(query).sort("created_at",-1); out=[]
    async for r in cur:
        patient=await get_db().users.find_one({"_id":r.get("patient_id")}) or {}
        out.append({"report_id":r["_id"],"analysis_id":r["analysis_id"],"patient_id":r.get("patient_id"),"patient_name":patient.get("full_name","Patient"),"created_at":r["created_at"].isoformat(),"status":r["status"]})
    return out
@router.post("/{report_id}/download/request")
async def request_download(report_id:str,bg:BackgroundTasks,user=Depends(get_current_user)):
    query={"_id":report_id}
    if user["role"] != "doctor": query["patient_id"] = user["user_id"]
    r=await get_db().reports.find_one(query)
    if not r: raise HTTPException(404,"Report not found")
    otp=await create_otp_challenge(user["user_id"],f"report_download:{report_id}"); bg.add_task(send_otp_email,user["email"],otp,"report download")
    return {"message":"OTP sent for report download"}
@router.post("/{report_id}/download/verify")
async def verify_download(report_id:str,otp:str,user=Depends(get_current_user)):
    query={"_id":report_id}
    if user["role"] != "doctor": query["patient_id"] = user["user_id"]
    r=await get_db().reports.find_one(query)
    if not r: raise HTTPException(404,"Report not found")
    if not await verify_otp_challenge(user["user_id"],f"report_download:{report_id}",otp): raise HTTPException(400,"Invalid or expired OTP")
    return FileResponse(r["file_path"],media_type="application/pdf",filename=f"PCOS-XAI-{report_id}.pdf")
```

from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
from uuid import uuid4

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException

from app.core.deps import get_current_user, require_roles
from app.db.mongo import get_db

router = APIRouter()


@router.get("/me")
async def me(user=Depends(get_current_user)):
    return {
        key: user.get(key)
        for key in [
            "user_id",
            "email",
            "phone",
            "full_name",
            "role",
            "verified"
        ]
    }


@router.post("/patient-access-code")
async def create_code(
    user=Depends(require_roles("patient"))
):
    code = f"PCOS-{str(uuid4())[:8].upper()}"

    expires_at = (
        datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        + timedelta(minutes=30)
    )

    await get_db().patient_access.insert_one({
        "code": code,
        "patient_id": user["user_id"],
        "expires_at": expires_at,
        "used": False
    })

    return {
        "access_code": code,
        "expires_at": expires_at
    }


@router.post("/doctor-access/{code}")
async def doctor_access(
    code: str,
    doctor=Depends(require_roles("doctor"))
):
    db = get_db()

    access_record = await db.patient_access.find_one({
        "code": code,
        "used": False
    })

    if not access_record:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Invalid or expired access code"
        )

    expires_at = access_record["expires_at"]

    # MongoDB may return a timezone-naive datetime.
    # Treat it as UTC before comparing.
    if expires_at.tzinfo is None:
        expires_at = expires_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    else:
        expires_at = expires_at.astimezone(timezone.utc)

    if expires_at <= datetime.now(timezone.utc):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Invalid or expired access code"
        )

    await db.patient_access.update_one(
        {
            "_id": access_record["_id"]
        },
        {
            "$set": {
                "used": True,
                "doctor_id": doctor["user_id"],
                "authorized_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            }
        }
    )

    return {
        "patient_id": access_record["patient_id"],
        "message": "Patient access granted"
    }

from fastapi import Depends,HTTPException
from fastapi.security import HTTPBearer,HTTPAuthorizationCredentials
from app.core.security import decode_token
from app.db.mongo import get_db
bearer=HTTPBearer()
async def get_current_user(credentials:HTTPAuthorizationCredentials=Depends(bearer)):
    p=decode_token(credentials.credentials)
    if not p or p.get("type")!="access": raise HTTPException(401,"Invalid or expired access token")
    user=await get_db().users.find_one({"_id":p["sub"]})
    if not user: raise HTTPException(401,"User not found")
    user["user_id"]=user["_id"]; return user
def require_roles(*roles):
    async def checker(user=Depends(get_current_user)):
        if user["role"] not in roles: raise HTTPException(403,"Insufficient permissions")
        return user
    return checker

from datetime import datetime,timedelta,timezone
from jose import jwt,JWTError
import bcrypt,secrets,hashlib
from app.core.config import settings

def hash_password(password): return bcrypt.hashpw(password.encode(),bcrypt.gensalt()).decode()
def verify_password(password,password_hash): return bcrypt.checkpw(password.encode(),password_hash.encode())
def create_token(subject,role,token_type,delta):
    now=datetime.now(timezone.utc); return jwt.encode({"sub":subject,"role":role,"type":token_type,"iat":now,"exp":now+delta},settings.JWT_SECRET,algorithm=settings.JWT_ALGORITHM)
def create_access_token(subject,role): return create_token(subject,role,"access",timedelta(minutes=settings.ACCESS_TOKEN_MINUTES))
def create_refresh_token(subject,role): return create_token(subject,role,"refresh",timedelta(days=settings.REFRESH_TOKEN_DAYS))
def decode_token(token):
    try: return jwt.decode(token,settings.JWT_SECRET,algorithms=[settings.JWT_ALGORITHM])
    except JWTError: return None
def generate_otp(): return f"{secrets.randbelow(1000000):06d}"
def hash_otp(otp): return hashlib.sha256(otp.encode()).hexdigest()
def verify_otp_hash(otp,otp_hash): return secrets.compare_digest(hash_otp(otp),otp_hash)

from motor.motor_asyncio import AsyncIOMotorClient
from app.core.config import settings
from uuid import uuid4

class MockCollection:
    def __init__(self, name):
        self.name = name
        self.store = []

    async def create_index(self, *args, **kwargs):
        pass

    async def drop_index(self, *args, **kwargs):
        pass

    async def index_information(self):
        return {}

    async def insert_one(self, doc):
        doc.setdefault("_id", str(uuid4()))
        self.store.append(doc)
        return type("InsertResult", (), {"inserted_id": doc.get("_id")})()

    async def delete_many(self, filter_doc):
        retained = [doc for doc in self.store if not all(doc.get(k) == v for k, v in filter_doc.items())]
        deleted_count = len(self.store) - len(retained)
        self.store = retained
        return type("DeleteResult", (), {"deleted_count": deleted_count})()

    async def update_one(self, filter_doc, update_doc):
        for doc in self.store:
            if all(doc.get(k) == v for k, v in filter_doc.items()):
                for key, value in update_doc.get("$set", {}).items():
                    doc[key] = value
                for key, value in update_doc.get("$inc", {}).items():
                    doc[key] = doc.get(key, 0) + value
                for key in update_doc.get("$unset", {}).keys():
                    doc.pop(key, None)
                return type("UpdateResult", (), {"modified_count": 1})()
        return type("UpdateResult", (), {"modified_count": 0})()

    async def update_many(self, filter_doc, update_doc):
        modified = 0
        for doc in self.store:
            if all(doc.get(k) == v for k, v in filter_doc.items()):
                for key, value in update_doc.get("$set", {}).items():
                    doc[key] = value
                for key, value in update_doc.get("$inc", {}).items():
                    doc[key] = doc.get(key, 0) + value
                for key in update_doc.get("$unset", {}).keys():
                    doc.pop(key, None)
                modified += 1
        return type("UpdateResult", (), {"modified_count": modified})()

    async def find_one(self, filter_doc):
        for doc in self.store:
            if all(doc.get(k) == v for k, v in filter_doc.items()):
                return doc
        return None

    def find(self, filter_doc=None):
        if filter_doc is None:
            filter_doc = {}
        results = [doc for doc in self.store if all(doc.get(k) == v for k, v in filter_doc.items())]
        class AsyncCursor:
            def __init__(self, items):
                self.items = items
            def sort(self, *args, **kwargs):
                return self
            async def to_list(self, length=None):
                return self.items[:length] if length else list(self.items)
            def __aiter__(self):
                self._iter = iter(self.items)
                return self
            async def __anext__(self):
                try:
                    return next(self._iter)
                except StopIteration:
                    raise StopAsyncIteration
        return AsyncCursor(results)

class MockDB:
    def __init__(self):
        self.collections = {}

    def __getattr__(self, name):
        if name not in self.collections:
            self.collections[name] = MockCollection(name)
        return self.collections[name]

    async def command(self, cmd, *args, **kwargs):
        return {"ok": 1}

mock_db_instance = MockDB()
client = None
db = mock_db_instance

async def connect_db():
    global client, db
    try:
        client = AsyncIOMotorClient(settings.MONGO_URI, serverSelectionTimeoutMS=2000)
        # Test connection ping
        await client.admin.command('ping')
        db = client[settings.DB_NAME]
        await db.users.create_index("email", unique=True)

        # Clean up any corrupt phone: null values from previous tests
        try:
            await db.users.update_many({"phone": None}, {"$unset": {"phone": ""}})
        except Exception:
            pass

        # Recreate phone index with partialFilterExpression to ignore missing/null phones
        try:
            indexes = await db.users.index_information()
            if "phone_1" in indexes:
                await db.users.drop_index("phone_1")
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            await db.users.create_index(
                "phone",
                unique=True,
                partialFilterExpression={"phone": {"$type": "string"}}
            )
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[MongoDB] Could not create partial index for phone: {e}")

        await db.otp_challenges.create_index("expires_at", expireAfterSeconds=0)
        await db.analyses.create_index([("patient_id", 1), ("created_at", -1)])
        await db.reports.create_index([("patient_id", 1), ("created_at", -1)])
        print("[MongoDB] Successfully connected to MongoDB.")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[MongoDB] Connection failed ({e}). Falling back to in-memory mock database.")
        db = mock_db_instance

async def close_db():
    global client
    if client:
        client.close()

def get_db():
    global db
    if db is None:
        return mock_db_instance
    return db
```

import importlib
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from PIL import Image

from app.core.config import settings

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]


class _ExplainerCache:
    clinical_explainer = None
    clinical_model = None
    image_explainer = None
    image_model = None


_cache = _ExplainerCache()


def _load_xai_module():
    project_root = str(PROJECT_ROOT)
    if project_root not in sys.path:
        sys.path.insert(0, project_root)
    return importlib.import_module("xai_pcos")


def _get_clinical_explainer(model):
    if (
        _cache.clinical_explainer is not None
        and _cache.clinical_model is model
    ):
        return _cache.clinical_explainer

    feature_names = list(model.feature_names_in_)
    dataset_path = (
        PROJECT_ROOT
        / "backend"
        / "model_weights"
        / "clinical"
        / "PCOS_data.csv"
    )
    background = pd.read_csv(dataset_path)
    background.columns = [str(name).strip() for name in background.columns]
    background = background[feature_names].apply(
        pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce"
    ).dropna()
    clinical_explainer = _load_xai_module().ClinicalExplainer
    _cache.clinical_explainer = clinical_explainer.from_model(
        model, background
    )
    _cache.clinical_model = model
    return _cache.clinical_explainer


def _get_image_explainer(model):
    if _cache.image_explainer is None or _cache.image_model is not model:
        xai = _load_xai_module()
        _cache.image_explainer = xai.TorchGradCAMPlusPlus(
            model,
            from_logits=True,
            pcos_index=settings.ULTRASOUND_PCOS_CLASS_INDEX,
        )
        _cache.image_model = model
    return _cache.image_explainer


def build_explanation(
    clinical_model,
    clinical_data,
    ultrasound_model,
    image_path=None,
    clinical_result=None,
):
    clinical = {
        "method": "SHAP",
        "status": "MODEL_NOT_CONNECTED",
        "top_features": [],
        "feature_importance": [],
    }
    if clinical_result and clinical_result.get("probability") is None:
        clinical = {
            "method": "SHAP (Random Forest)",
            "status": clinical_result.get("prediction", "NOT_AVAILABLE"),
            "top_features": [],
            "feature_importance": [],
            "message": clinical_result.get(
                "message", "Clinical explanation is not available."
            ),
        }
    elif clinical_model.model is not None:
        try:
            feature_frame = clinical_model.prepare_features(clinical_data)
            result = _get_clinical_explainer(
                clinical_model.model
            ).explain_patient(feature_frame.iloc[0].to_dict())
            clinical = {
                "method": "SHAP (Random Forest)",
                "status": "SUCCESS",
                **result,
            }
        except Exception as error:
            clinical = clinical_model.explain(clinical_data)
            clinical["status"] = "FALLBACK_GLOBAL_IMPORTANCE"
            clinical["error"] = str(error)

    ultrasound = {
        "method": "Grad-CAM++",
        "status": "NOT_AVAILABLE",
    }
    if image_path:
        if ultrasound_model.model is None:
            ultrasound = {
                "method": "Grad-CAM++",
                "status": "MODEL_NOT_CONNECTED",
            }
        else:
            try:
                tensor = ultrasound_model.preprocess_image(image_path)
                with Image.open(image_path) as image:
                    rgb = np.asarray(image.convert("RGB"))
                image_result = _load_xai_module().explain_image(
                    _get_image_explainer(ultrasound_model.model),
                    tensor,
                    rgb,
                )
                ultrasound = {
                    "method": "Grad-CAM++ (EfficientNet-B0)",
                    "status": "SUCCESS",
                    **image_result,
                }
            except Exception as error:
                ultrasound = {
                    "method": "Grad-CAM++ (EfficientNet-B0)",
                    "status": "ERROR",
                    "error": str(error),
                }

    explanation = {
        "clinical": clinical,
        "ultrasound": ultrasound,
        "summary": (
            "SHAP shows per-patient clinical feature effects; Grad-CAM++ "
            "highlights image regions used by the ultrasound model. These "
            "are model explanations, not medical causation."
        ),
    }
    clinical_probability = clinical.get("probability_pcos")
    image_probability = ultrasound.get("probability_pcos")
    if clinical_probability is not None and image_probability is not None:
        explanation["fusion"] = {
            "method": "equal_weight_probability_average",
            "probability_pcos": round(
                (clinical_probability + image_probability) / 2, 4
            ),
            "clinical_contribution": round(
                (clinical_probability - 0.5) / 2, 4
            ),
            "ultrasound_contribution": round(
                (image_probability - 0.5) / 2, 4
            ),
        }
    return explanation
```

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from app.core.config import settings
from app.db.mongo import connect_db, close_db
from app.api import auth, users, analysis, reports, health

app = FastAPI(title=settings.APP_NAME, version="1.0.0", description="Portable multimodal PCOS Explainable AI backend")
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=[
        "http://localhost:3000",
        "http://localhost:5173",
        "http://localhost:5174",
        "http://localhost:5175",
        "http://localhost:5176",
        "http://localhost:4173",
        "http://127.0.0.1:3000",
        "http://127.0.0.1:5173",
        "http://127.0.0.1:5174",
        "http://127.0.0.1:5175",
        "http://127.0.0.1:5176",
        "http://127.0.0.1:4173",
    ],
    allow_origin_regex=r"^https?://(localhost|127\.0\.0\.1)(:\d+)?$",
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

@app.on_event("startup")
async def startup(): await connect_db()
@app.on_event("shutdown")
async def shutdown(): await close_db()

app.include_router(health.router, prefix="/api")
app.include_router(auth.router, prefix="/api/auth", tags=["Authentication"])
app.include_router(users.router, prefix="/api/users", tags=["Users"])
app.include_router(analysis.router, prefix="/api/analysis", tags=["AI Analysis"])
app.include_router(reports.router, prefix="/api/reports", tags=["Reports"])

@app.get("/")
async def root(): return {"service":settings.APP_NAME,"status":"running","docs":"/docs"}

import joblib
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np
from pathlib import Path

MODEL_PATH = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent.parent / "model_weights" / "clinical" / "pcos_model.pkl"
FEATURES_PATH = MODEL_PATH.parent / "pcos_features.pkl"

DEFAULT_FEATURE_VALUES = {
    'Age (yrs)': 27.0,
    'Weight (Kg)': 60.0,
    'Height(Cm)': 162.0,
    'BMI': 22.8,
    'Blood Group': 11.0,
    'Pulse rate(bpm)': 72.0,
    'RR (breaths/min)': 18.0,
    'Hb(g/dl)': 12.5,
    'Cycle(R/I)': 2.0,  # 2: regular, 4: irregular
    'Cycle length(days)': 5.0,
    'Marraige Status (Yrs)': 3.0,
    'Pregnant(Y/N)': 0.0,
    'No. of abortions': 0.0,
    'I   beta-HCG(mIU/mL)': 1.99,
    'II    beta-HCG(mIU/mL)': 1.99,
    'FSH(mIU/mL)': 5.5,
    'LH(mIU/mL)': 4.2,
    'FSH/LH': 1.3,
    'Hip(inch)': 36.0,
    'Waist(inch)': 30.0,
    'Waist:Hip Ratio': 0.83,
    'TSH (mIU/L)': 2.5,
    'AMH(ng/mL)': 3.5,
    'PRL(ng/mL)': 18.0,
    'Vit D3 (ng/mL)': 25.0,
    'PRG(ng/mL)': 0.5,
    'RBS(mg/dl)': 95.0,
    'Weight gain(Y/N)': 0.0,
    'hair growth(Y/N)': 0.0,
    'Skin darkening (Y/N)': 0.0,
    'Hair loss(Y/N)': 0.0,
    'Pimples(Y/N)': 0.0,
    'Fast food (Y/N)': 0.0,
    'Reg.Exercise(Y/N)': 1.0,
    'BP _Systolic (mmHg)': 120.0,
    'BP _Diastolic (mmHg)': 80.0,
    'Follicle No. (L)': 4.0,
    'Follicle No. (R)': 4.0,
    'Avg. F size (L) (mm)': 15.0,
    'Avg. F size (R) (mm)': 15.0,
    'Endometrium (mm)': 7.5
}

REQUIRED_INPUTS = (
    "age", "weight", "height", "bmi", "blood_group", "pulse_rate", "respiratory_rate", "hb", "cycle", "cycle_length",
    "marriage_status_years", "pregnant", "abortions", "beta_hcg_1", "beta_hcg_2", "fsh", "lh", "fsh_lh",
    "hip", "waist", "waist_hip_ratio", "tsh", "amh", "prolactin", "vitamin_d3", "progesterone", "rbs",
    "weight_gain", "hair_growth", "skin_darkening", "hair_loss", "pimples", "fast_food", "regular_exercise",
    "bp_systolic", "bp_diastolic", "follicle_no_left", "follicle_no_right", "average_follicle_size_left",
    "average_follicle_size_right", "endometrium",
)

class ClinicalModel:
    model_version = "RandomForest-Clinical-v1"
    computed_inputs = {"bmi", "fsh_lh", "waist_hip_ratio"}
    input_aliases = {
        "cycle": ("cycle", "irregular_cycles", "irregularPeriods"),
        "marriage_status_years": ("marriage_status_years", "maritalStatus"),
        "hair_growth": ("hair_growth", "excessiveHair"),
        "hair_loss": ("hair_loss", "hairLoss"),
        "pimples": ("pimples", "acne"),
    }

    def __init__(self):
        self.model = None
        self.feature_names = None
        self._load_model()

    def _load_model(self):
        try:
            if MODEL_PATH.exists():
                self.model = joblib.load(MODEL_PATH)
                if FEATURES_PATH.exists():
                    saved_features = joblib.load(FEATURES_PATH)
                    if isinstance(saved_features, list):
                        self.feature_names = saved_features
                if self.feature_names is None and hasattr(self.model, "feature_names_in_"):
                    self.feature_names = list(self.model.feature_names_in_)
                print(f"[ClinicalModel] Successfully loaded model from {MODEL_PATH}")
            else:
                print(f"[ClinicalModel] Model file not found at {MODEL_PATH}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[ClinicalModel] Error loading model: {e}")
            self.model = None

    def _map_input_to_features(self, clinical_data: dict) -> pd.DataFrame:
        features = DEFAULT_FEATURE_VALUES.copy()
        
        if not clinical_data:
            clinical_data = {}

        # Basic Info
        if clinical_data.get("age") is not None: features['Age (yrs)'] = float(clinical_data["age"])
        if clinical_data.get("weight") is not None: features['Weight (Kg)'] = float(clinical_data["weight"])
        if clinical_data.get("height") is not None: features['Height(Cm)'] = float(clinical_data["height"])
        
        # Match the notebook: use the entered BMI, with calculation only as a fallback.
        if clinical_data.get("bmi") is not None:
            features['BMI'] = float(clinical_data["bmi"])
        elif clinical_data.get("height") and clinical_data.get("weight"):
            h_m = float(clinical_data["height"]) / 100.0
            if h_m > 0:
                features['BMI'] = float(clinical_data["weight"]) / (h_m * h_m)

        if clinical_data.get("blood_group") is not None: features['Blood Group'] = float(clinical_data["blood_group"])
        if clinical_data.get("pulse_rate") is not None: features['Pulse rate(bpm)'] = float(clinical_data["pulse_rate"])
        if clinical_data.get("respiratory_rate") is not None: features['RR (breaths/min)'] = float(clinical_data["respiratory_rate"])
        if clinical_data.get("hb") is not None: features['Hb(g/dl)'] = float(clinical_data["hb"])

        # Menstrual / Clinical
        if clinical_data.get("cycle_length") is not None: features['Cycle length(days)'] = float(clinical_data["cycle_length"])
        
        # Irregular periods boolean / string
        irreg = clinical_data.get("irregular_cycles") or clinical_data.get("cycle") or clinical_data.get("irregularPeriods")
        if irreg is not None:
            if isinstance(irreg, str):
                features['Cycle(R/I)'] = 4.0 if irreg.lower() == "yes" or irreg.lower() == "irregular" else 2.0
            else:
                features['Cycle(R/I)'] = 4.0 if bool(irreg) else 2.0

        if clinical_data.get("marriage_status_years") is not None:
            features['Marraige Status (Yrs)'] = float(clinical_data["marriage_status_years"])
        elif clinical_data.get("maritalStatus") is not None:
            features['Marraige Status (Yrs)'] = 3.0 if str(clinical_data["maritalStatus"]).lower() == "married" else 0.0

        if clinical_data.get("pregnant") is not None: features['Pregnant(Y/N)'] = 1.0 if bool(clinical_data["pregnant"]) else 0.0
        if clinical_data.get("abortions") is not None: features['No. of abortions'] = float(clinical_data["abortions"])
        if clinical_data.get("beta_hcg_1") is not None: features['I   beta-HCG(mIU/mL)'] = float(clinical_data["beta_hcg_1"])
        if clinical_data.get("beta_hcg_2") is not None: features['II    beta-HCG(mIU/mL)'] = float(clinical_data["beta_hcg_2"])

        # Hormonal
        if clinical_data.get("prolactin") is not None: features['PRL(ng/mL)'] = float(clinical_data["prolactin"])
        if clinical_data.get("lh") is not None: features['LH(mIU/mL)'] = float(clinical_data["lh"])
        if clinical_data.get("fsh") is not None: features['FSH(mIU/mL)'] = float(clinical_data["fsh"])
        if clinical_data.get("fsh_lh") is not None: features['FSH/LH'] = float(clinical_data["fsh_lh"])
        elif features['FSH(mIU/mL)'] and features['LH(mIU/mL)'] and features['LH(mIU/mL)'] > 0:
            features['FSH/LH'] = features['FSH(mIU/mL)'] / features['LH(mIU/mL)']
            
        if clinical_data.get("amh") is not None: features['AMH(ng/mL)'] = float(clinical_data["amh"])
        if clinical_data.get("tsh") is not None: features['TSH (mIU/L)'] = float(clinical_data["tsh"])
        if clinical_data.get("vitamin_d3") is not None: features['Vit D3 (ng/mL)'] = float(clinical_data["vitamin_d3"])
        if clinical_data.get("progesterone") is not None: features['PRG(ng/mL)'] = float(clinical_data["progesterone"])
        if clinical_data.get("rbs") is not None: features['RBS(mg/dl)'] = float(clinical_data["rbs"])

        # Symptoms
        def to_binary(val):
            if val is None: return 0.0
            if isinstance(val, str): return 1.0 if val.lower() in ["yes", "true", "1"] else 0.0
            return 1.0 if bool(val) else 0.0

        if "weight_gain" in clinical_data: features['Weight gain(Y/N)'] = to_binary(clinical_data["weight_gain"])
        if "hair_growth" in clinical_data or "excessiveHair" in clinical_data:
            features['hair growth(Y/N)'] = to_binary(clinical_data.get("hair_growth") or clinical_data.get("excessiveHair"))
        if "skin_darkening" in clinical_data: features['Skin darkening (Y/N)'] = to_binary(clinical_data["skin_darkening"])
        if "hair_loss" in clinical_data or "hairLoss" in clinical_data:
            features['Hair loss(Y/N)'] = to_binary(clinical_data.get("hair_loss") or clinical_data.get("hairLoss"))
        if "pimples" in clinical_data or "acne" in clinical_data:
            features['Pimples(Y/N)'] = to_binary(clinical_data.get("pimples") or clinical_data.get("acne"))
        if "fast_food" in clinical_data: features['Fast food (Y/N)'] = to_binary(clinical_data["fast_food"])
        if "regular_exercise" in clinical_data: features['Reg.Exercise(Y/N)'] = to_binary(clinical_data["regular_exercise"])
        if clinical_data.get("bp_systolic") is not None: features['BP _Systolic (mmHg)'] = float(clinical_data["bp_systolic"])
        if clinical_data.get("bp_diastolic") is not None: features['BP _Diastolic (mmHg)'] = float(clinical_data["bp_diastolic"])

        if clinical_data.get("hip") is not None: features['Hip(inch)'] = float(clinical_data["hip"])
        if clinical_data.get("waist") is not None: features['Waist(inch)'] = float(clinical_data["waist"])
        if clinical_data.get("waist_hip_ratio") is not None:
            features['Waist:Hip Ratio'] = float(clinical_data["waist_hip_ratio"])
        elif clinical_data.get("waist") and clinical_data.get("hip"):
            features['Waist:Hip Ratio'] = float(clinical_data["waist"]) / float(clinical_data["hip"])

        # Follicle / Ultrasound tabular
        if clinical_data.get("follicle_no_left") is not None: features['Follicle No. (L)'] = float(clinical_data["follicle_no_left"])
        if clinical_data.get("follicle_no_right") is not None: features['Follicle No. (R)'] = float(clinical_data["follicle_no_right"])
        if clinical_data.get("average_follicle_size_left") is not None: features['Avg. F size (L) (mm)'] = float(clinical_data["average_follicle_size_left"])
        if clinical_data.get("average_follicle_size_right") is not None: features['Avg. F size (R) (mm)'] = float(clinical_data["average_follicle_size_right"])
        if clinical_data.get("endometrium") is not None: features['Endometrium (mm)'] = float(clinical_data["endometrium"])

        # Build DataFrame with order matching model feature_names_in_
        if self.feature_names:
            cols = self.feature_names
        elif self.model and hasattr(self.model, "feature_names_in_"):
            cols = list(self.model.feature_names_in_)
        else:
            cols = list(DEFAULT_FEATURE_VALUES.keys())

        df = pd.DataFrame([{col: features.get(col, DEFAULT_FEATURE_VALUES.get(col, 0.0)) for col in cols}])
        return df

    def prepare_features(self, clinical_data: dict) -> pd.DataFrame:
        """Return the encoded clinical input used by the model."""
        return self._map_input_to_features(clinical_data)

    @staticmethod
    def _has_input(clinical_data: dict, keys: tuple[str, ...]) -> bool:
        return any(
            clinical_data.get(key) is not None and clinical_data.get(key) != ""
            for key in keys
        )

    def missing_inputs(self, clinical_data: dict) -> list[str]:
        """List required form inputs, allowing the three derived values."""
        values = clinical_data or {}
        missing = []
        for key in REQUIRED_INPUTS:
            if key in self.computed_inputs:
                continue
            aliases = self.input_aliases.get(key, (key,))
            if not self._has_input(values, aliases):
                missing.append(key)

        derived_sources = {
            "bmi": ("weight", "height"),
            "fsh_lh": ("fsh", "lh"),
            "waist_hip_ratio": ("waist", "hip"),
        }
        for key, sources in derived_sources.items():
            if self._has_input(values, (key,)):
                continue
            if not self._has_input(values, sources):
                missing.append(key)
                continue
            denominator = sources[1]
            try:
                if float(values[denominator]) <= 0:
                    missing.append(key)
            except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
                missing.append(key)
        return missing

    def predict(self, clinical_data: dict):
        missing = self.missing_inputs(clinical_data)
        if missing:
            return {
                "prediction": "NOT_AVAILABLE",
                "probability": None,
                "model_version": self.model_version,
                "message": "Clinical estimate unavailable until all required values are provided.",
                "missing_fields": missing,
            }

        if not self.model:
            self._load_model()
            if not self.model:
                return {
                    "prediction": "UNAVAILABLE",
                    "probability": None,
                    "model_version": self.model_version,
                    "message": "Clinical model is unavailable.",
                }

        try:
            df = self._map_input_to_features(clinical_data)
            probabilities = self.model.predict_proba(df)[0]
            # Random Forest output index 1 represents PCOS probability
            pcos_prob = float(probabilities[1]) if len(probabilities) > 1 else float(probabilities[0])
            pred_label = "PCOS" if pcos_prob >= 0.5 else "Normal"
            binary_inputs = {
                "Pregnant(Y/N)": "pregnant",
                "Weight gain(Y/N)": "weight_gain",
                "hair growth(Y/N)": "hair_growth",
                "Skin darkening (Y/N)": "skin_darkening",
                "Hair loss(Y/N)": "hair_loss",
                "Pimples(Y/N)": "pimples",
                "Fast food (Y/N)": "fast_food",
                "Reg.Exercise(Y/N)": "regular_exercise",
            }
            encoded_values = {}
            for column, value in df.iloc[0].items():
                encoded_value = value.item() if hasattr(value, "item") else value
                if column == "Cycle(R/I)":
                    display_label = "Irregular" if encoded_value == 4 else "Regular"
                elif column in binary_inputs:
                    display_label = "Yes" if encoded_value == 1 else "No"
                else:
                    display_label = str(encoded_value)
                encoded_values[column] = {"label": display_label, "encoded_value": encoded_value}

            return {
                "prediction": pred_label,
                "probability": round(pcos_prob, 4),
                "encoded_values": encoded_values,
                "model_version": self.model_version
            }
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[ClinicalModel] Prediction error: {e}")
            return {"prediction": "ERROR", "probability": None, "error": str(e), "model_version": self.model_version}

    def explain(self, clinical_data: dict):
        missing = self.missing_inputs(clinical_data)
        if missing:
            return {
                "method": "SHAP",
                "feature_importance": [],
                "status": "NOT_AVAILABLE",
                "message": "Clinical explanation unavailable until all required values are provided.",
            }
        if not self.model:
            return {"method": "Feature Importance", "feature_importance": [], "status": "MODEL_NOT_CONNECTED"}

        try:
            df = self._map_input_to_features(clinical_data)
            importances = self.model.feature_importances_
            feature_names = df.columns.tolist()

            zipped = sorted(zip(feature_names, importances, df.iloc[0].values), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
            top_features = [
                {"feature": f, "importance": round(float(imp), 4), "value": float(val)}
                for f, imp, val in zipped[:8]
            ]

            return {
                "method": "RandomForest Feature Importance",
                "feature_importance": top_features,
                "status": "SUCCESS"
            }
        except Exception as e:
            return {"method": "Feature Importance", "feature_importance": [], "status": f"ERROR: {e}"}

clinical_model = ClinicalModel()
