### FILE: frontend/src/lib/api.js  (183 lines)
```
// Every call to the FastAPI backend lives here. The pages never call fetch themselves.
//
// Endpoints used (see the Backend Spec):
//   POST /api/auth/register, /verify-registration, /login, /verify-login, /refresh
//   GET  /api/users/me
//   POST /api/users/patient-access-code, /api/users/doctor-access/{code}
//   POST /api/analysis/run            multipart: clinical_json (text) + ultrasound (optional file)
//   GET  /api/analysis/history, /doctor-history, /{analysis_id}
//   POST /api/reports/{analysis_id}/generate
//   GET  /api/reports
//   POST /api/reports/{report_id}/download/request, /download/verify?otp=...   (PDF)

import { API_URL } from '../config'

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(message, status = 0) {
    super(message)
    this.name = 'ApiError'
    this.status = status
  }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- tokens

const KEY = 'pcos.session'
let tokens = null
let onExpired = () => {}

try {
  tokens = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY) || 'null')
} catch {
  tokens = null
}

export const getTokens = () => tokens

export function setTokens(next) {
  tokens = ***REDACTED***
  try {
    if (next) localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(next))
    else localStorage.removeItem(KEY)
  } catch {
    // Storage can be blocked (private windows). The session then lasts until the tab closes.
  }
}

export const setExpiredHandler = (fn) => {
  onExpired = fn
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- plumbing

// FastAPI sends { detail: "text" } or, for validation errors, { detail: [{ loc, msg }] }.
function readDetail(body, status) {
  const d = body?.detail
  if (typeof d === 'string') return d
  if (Array.isArray(d)) {
    return d
      .map((e) => {
        const field = Array.isArray(e.loc) ? e.loc.filter((x) => x !== 'body').join('.') : ''
        return field ? `${field}: ${e.msg}` : e.msg
      })
      .join('; ')
  }
  if (status === 413) return 'That file is too large.'
  if (status >= 500) return 'The server had a problem. Try again in a moment.'
  return `The server answered ${status}.`
}

async function send(path, { method = 'GET', json, form, query, auth = true, accessToken }) {
  const url = new URL(`${API_URL}${path}`)
  if (query) Object.entries(query).forEach(([k, v]) => url.searchParams.set(k, v))
  const headers = {}
  let body
  if (json !== undefined) {
    headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json'
    body = JSON.stringify(json)
  } else if (form) {
    body = form // the browser sets the multipart boundary itself
  }
  const token = ***REDACTED*** ?? tokens?.access
  if (auth && token) headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`
  try {
    return await fetch(url, { method, headers, body })
  } catch {
    throw new ApiError(
      `Cannot reach the server at ${API_URL}. Check that the backend is running and that it allows requests from this address (CORS).`,
    )
  }
}

let refreshing = null
function refreshAccess() {
  if (!tokens?.refresh) return Promise.resolve(null)
  refreshing ??= (async () => {
    try {
      const res = await send('/api/auth/refresh', { method: 'POST', json: { refresh_token: ***REDACTED*** }, auth: false })
      if (!res.ok) return null
      const data = await res.json()
      setTokens({ ...tokens, access: data.access_token })
      return data.access_token
    } catch {
      return null
    } finally {
      refreshing = null
    }
  })()
  return refreshing
}

async function request(path, opts = {}) {
  let res = await send(path, opts)
  const wantsAuth = opts.auth !== false
  // FastAPI's bearer scheme answers a missing or malformed header with 403 "Not authenticated".
  // That and a 401 both mean the session needs renewing. Other 403s are real permission errors.
  let needsAuth = res.status === 401
  if (!needsAuth && res.status === 403) {
    const body = await res.clone().json().catch(() => null)
    needsAuth = body?.detail === 'Not authenticated'
  }
  if (wantsAuth && needsAuth) {
    const fresh = await refreshAccess()
    if (fresh) res = await send(path, { ...opts, accessToken: ***REDACTED*** })
    if (!fresh || res.status === 401) {
      setTokens(null)
      onExpired()
      throw new ApiError('Your session has expired. Sign in again.', 401)
    }
  }
  if (!res.ok) {
    let body = null
    try {
      body = await res.json()
    } catch {
      // not JSON
    }
    throw new ApiError(readDetail(body, res.status), res.status)
  }
  return opts.blob ? res.blob() : res.json()
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- account

export const register = (data) => request('/api/auth/register', { method: 'POST', json: data, auth: false })
export const verifyRegistration = (user_id, otp) =>
  request('/api/auth/verify-registration', { method: 'POST', json: { user_id, otp }, auth: false })
export const login = (email, password) =>
  request('/api/auth/login', { method: 'POST', json: { email, password }, auth: false })
export const verifyLogin = (user_id, otp) =>
  request('/api/auth/verify-login', { method: 'POST', json: { user_id, otp }, auth: false })
export const getMe = () => request('/api/users/me')

export const createAccessCode = () => request('/api/users/patient-access-code', { method: 'POST' })
export const redeemAccessCode = (code) =>
  request(`/api/users/doctor-access/${encodeURIComponent(code.trim())}`, { method: 'POST' })

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- analysis

// `clinical` is the payload object from buildPayload(); `image` is an optional File.
export function runAnalysis({ clinical, image }) {
  const form = new FormData()
  form.append('clinical_json', JSON.stringify(clinical ?? {}))
  if (image) form.append('ultrasound', image, image.name)
  return request('/api/analysis/run', { method: 'POST', form })
}

export const getHistory = () => request('/api/analysis/history')
export const getDoctorHistory = () => request('/api/analysis/doctor-history')
export const getAnalysis = (id) => request(`/api/analysis/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`)

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- reports

export const generateReport = (analysisId) =>
  request(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(analysisId)}/generate`, { method: 'POST' })
export const listReports = () => request('/api/reports')
export const requestDownload = (reportId) =>
  request(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(reportId)}/download/request`, { method: 'POST' })
export const verifyDownload = (reportId, otp) =>
  request(`/api/reports/${encodeURIComponent(reportId)}/download/verify`, {
    method: 'POST',
    query: { otp },
    blob: true,
  })
```

### FILE: frontend/src/lib/auth.jsx  (56 lines)
```
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { getMe, getTokens, setExpiredHandler, setTokens } from './api'

// Who is signed in. `status` is 'loading' while a saved session is being checked, then 'in' or 'out'.
// user = { user_id, role: 'patient' | 'doctor', full_name, email? }

const AuthContext = createContext(null)

export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState(null)
  const [status, setStatus] = useState(() => (getTokens()?.access ? 'loading' : 'out'))

  const signOut = useCallback(() => {
    setTokens(null)
    setUser(null)
    setStatus('out')
  }, [])

  // The API layer calls this when a token can no longer be refreshed.
  useEffect(() => {
    setExpiredHandler(() => {
      setUser(null)
      setStatus('out')
    })
  }, [])

  // Restore a saved session on page load.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!getTokens()?.access) return
    let alive = true
    getMe()
      .then((me) => {
        if (!alive) return
        setUser({ user_id: me.user_id, role: me.role, full_name: me.full_name, email: me.email })
        setStatus('in')
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (alive) signOut()
      })
    return () => {
      alive = false
    }
  }, [signOut])

  // `data` is the body of verify-registration / verify-login.
  const signIn = useCallback((data) => {
    setTokens({ access: data.access_token, refresh: data.refresh_token })
    setUser({ user_id: data.user_id, role: data.role, full_name: data.full_name })
    setStatus('in')
  }, [])

  const value = useMemo(() => ({ user, status, signIn, signOut }), [user, status, signIn, signOut])
  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>
}

export const useAuth = () => useContext(AuthContext)
```

### FILE: frontend/src/lib/features.js  (187 lines)
```
// The clinical inputs, taken from the backend's feature table (Backend Spec, section 3).
// `key` is the field name the API accepts, `column` is the name of the matching model column.
// The form, the validation, the request payload and the result tables all read this one list.
//
// min/max are sanity limits for typing mistakes. The backend itself checks no ranges, so these only
// stop obvious slips such as a height of 16 cm. Widen them if your data needs it.
//
// BMI, FSH/LH and waist:hip ratio are not asked for. The server works them out from the values
// above (weight and height, FSH and LH, waist and hip), so they are listed separately in COMPUTED.

import { YES_NO_FORMAT } from '../config'

export const GROUPS = [
  { id: 'body', title: 'Body and vitals', note: 'Measurements from a routine check.' },
  { id: 'cycle', title: 'Cycle and history', note: 'Periods, pregnancy and related history.' },
  { id: 'labs', title: 'Hormones and blood tests', note: 'Lab results, if you have them.' },
  { id: 'signs', title: 'Symptoms and lifestyle', note: 'Yes or no. Leave unanswered if unknown.' },
  { id: 'us', title: 'Follicles and endometrium', note: 'Measurements from the ultrasound report, if you have them.' },
]

// Blood group codes follow the public Kaggle PCOS dataset. Confirm them against your training data.
export const BLOOD_GROUPS = [
  { value: '11', label: 'A+' },
  { value: '12', label: 'A−' },
  { value: '13', label: 'B+' },
  { value: '14', label: 'B−' },
  { value: '15', label: 'O+' },
  { value: '16', label: 'O−' },
  { value: '17', label: 'AB+' },
  { value: '18', label: 'AB−' },
]

const num = (key, column, group, label, unit, min, max, step, extra = {}) => ({
  key, column, group, kind: 'number', label, unit, min, max, step, ...extra,
})
const yn = (key, column, label, extra = {}) => ({ key, column, group: 'signs', kind: 'yesno', label, ...extra })

export const FEATURES = [
  num('age', 'Age (yrs)', 'body', 'Age', 'years', 10, 70, 1),
  num('weight', 'Weight (Kg)', 'body', 'Weight', 'kg', 25, 200, 0.1),
  num('height', 'Height(Cm)', 'body', 'Height', 'cm', 120, 210, 0.1),
  { key: 'blood_group', column: 'Blood Group', group: 'body', kind: 'select', label: 'Blood group', options: BLOOD_GROUPS },
  num('pulse_rate', 'Pulse rate(bpm)', 'body', 'Pulse rate', 'bpm', 40, 200, 1),
  num('respiratory_rate', 'RR (breaths/min)', 'body', 'Respiratory rate', 'per min', 8, 40, 1),
  num('hb', 'Hb(g/dl)', 'body', 'Haemoglobin', 'g/dL', 4, 20, 0.1),
  num('bp_systolic', 'BP _Systolic (mmHg)', 'body', 'Blood pressure, systolic', 'mmHg', 70, 220, 1),
  num('bp_diastolic', 'BP _Diastolic (mmHg)', 'body', 'Blood pressure, diastolic', 'mmHg', 40, 140, 1),
  num('waist', 'Waist(inch)', 'body', 'Waist', 'inch', 18, 70, 0.1),
  num('hip', 'Hip(inch)', 'body', 'Hip', 'inch', 20, 70, 0.1),

  {
    key: 'cycle', column: 'Cycle(R/I)', group: 'cycle', kind: 'choice', label: 'Cycle pattern',
    options: [
      { value: 'regular', label: 'Regular' },
      { value: 'irregular', label: 'Irregular' },
    ],
  },
  num('cycle_length', 'Cycle length(days)', 'cycle', 'Cycle length', 'days', 1, 60, 1, {
    hint: 'The training data mostly holds values of about 2 to 12, so this is probably the days of bleeding, not the gap between periods.',
  }),
  num('marriage_status_years', 'Marraige Status (Yrs)', 'cycle', 'Years married', 'years', 0, 50, 1),
  { key: 'pregnant', column: 'Pregnant(Y/N)', group: 'cycle', kind: 'yesno', label: 'Currently pregnant' },
  num('abortions', 'No. of abortions', 'cycle', 'Number of abortions', 'count', 0, 10, 1),

  num('fsh', 'FSH(mIU/mL)', 'labs', 'FSH', 'mIU/mL', 0, 200, 0.1),
  num('lh', 'LH(mIU/mL)', 'labs', 'LH', 'mIU/mL', 0, 200, 0.1),
  num('amh', 'AMH(ng/mL)', 'labs', 'AMH', 'ng/mL', 0, 100, 0.01),
  num('tsh', 'TSH (mIU/L)', 'labs', 'TSH', 'mIU/L', 0, 100, 0.01),
  num('prolactin', 'PRL(ng/mL)', 'labs', 'Prolactin', 'ng/mL', 0, 500, 0.1),
  num('vitamin_d3', 'Vit D3 (ng/mL)', 'labs', 'Vitamin D3', 'ng/mL', 0, 200, 0.1),
  num('progesterone', 'PRG(ng/mL)', 'labs', 'Progesterone', 'ng/mL', 0, 100, 0.01),
  num('rbs', 'RBS(mg/dl)', 'labs', 'Random blood sugar', 'mg/dL', 20, 600, 1),
  num('beta_hcg_1', 'I   beta-HCG(mIU/mL)', 'labs', 'β-hCG, first test', 'mIU/mL', 0, 500000, 0.01),
  num('beta_hcg_2', 'II    beta-HCG(mIU/mL)', 'labs', 'β-hCG, second test', 'mIU/mL', 0, 500000, 0.01),

  yn('weight_gain', 'Weight gain(Y/N)', 'Recent weight gain'),
  yn('hair_growth', 'hair growth(Y/N)', 'Excess hair growth', { aliases: ['excessiveHair'] }),
  yn('skin_darkening', 'Skin darkening (Y/N)', 'Skin darkening'),
  yn('hair_loss', 'Hair loss(Y/N)', 'Hair loss', { aliases: ['hairLoss'] }),
  yn('pimples', 'Pimples(Y/N)', 'Pimples or acne', { aliases: ['acne'] }),
  yn('fast_food', 'Fast food (Y/N)', 'Eats fast food regularly'),
  yn('regular_exercise', 'Reg.Exercise(Y/N)', 'Exercises regularly'),

  num('follicle_no_left', 'Follicle No. (L)', 'us', 'Follicle count, left ovary', 'count', 0, 40, 1),
  num('follicle_no_right', 'Follicle No. (R)', 'us', 'Follicle count, right ovary', 'count', 0, 40, 1),
  num('average_follicle_size_left', 'Avg. F size (L) (mm)', 'us', 'Average follicle size, left', 'mm', 0, 40, 0.1),
  num('average_follicle_size_right', 'Avg. F size (R) (mm)', 'us', 'Average follicle size, right', 'mm', 0, 40, 0.1),
  num('endometrium', 'Endometrium (mm)', 'us', 'Endometrium thickness', 'mm', 0, 30, 0.1),
]

// Worked out by the server when you do not send them.
export const COMPUTED = [
  { key: 'bmi', column: 'BMI', label: 'BMI', unit: 'kg/m²', from: 'weight and height', compute: (v) => v.weight && v.height ? v.weight / (v.height / 100) ** 2 : null },
  { key: 'fsh_lh', column: 'FSH/LH', label: 'FSH/LH', unit: '', from: 'FSH and LH', compute: (v) => v.fsh != null && v.lh > 0 ? v.fsh / v.lh : null },
  { key: 'waist_hip_ratio', column: 'Waist:Hip Ratio', label: 'Waist:hip ratio', unit: '', from: 'waist and hip', compute: (v) => v.waist && v.hip ? v.waist / v.hip : null },
]

export const ALL_FEATURES = [...FEATURES, ...COMPUTED]

const norm = (s) => String(s).toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()
const byColumn = new Map(ALL_FEATURES.map((f) => [norm(f.column), f]))
export const featureForColumn = (column) => byColumn.get(norm(column))

export const emptyClinical = () => Object.fromEntries(FEATURES.map((f) => [f.key, '']))

// Turns the raw form strings into clean values plus per-field errors.
export function parseClinical(values) {
  const valid = {}
  const errors = {}
  for (const f of FEATURES) {
    const raw = values[f.key]
    if (raw === '' || raw === undefined || raw === null) continue
    if (f.kind === 'number') {
      const n = Number(raw)
      if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n < f.min || n > f.max) errors[f.key] = `Enter a value from ${f.min} to ${f.max}.`
      else valid[f.key] = n
    } else if (f.kind === 'select') {
      valid[f.key] = Number(raw)
    } else {
      valid[f.key] = raw
    }
  }
  return { valid, errors }
}

export const countProvided = (values) => Object.keys(parseClinical(values).valid).length

// The object sent as `clinical_json`. The API does not estimate clinically until all form fields are present.
export function buildPayload(values) {
  const { valid } = parseClinical(values)
  const out = {}
  for (const f of FEATURES) {
    if (!(f.key in valid)) continue
    const v = valid[f.key]
    if (f.kind === 'yesno') out[f.key] = YES_NO_FORMAT === 'string' ? v : v === 'yes'
    else out[f.key] = v
  }
  return out
}

// Values the server computed or defaulted can be told apart from entered ones by looking at what
// was saved with the analysis (`clinical_input`, with null for anything not sent).
const sent = (input, f) => [f.key, ...(f.aliases ?? [])].some((k) => input?.[k] != null && input[k] !== '')

export function wasEntered(f, input) {
  if (f.key === 'cycle') return ['cycle', 'irregular_cycles', 'irregularPeriods'].some((k) => input?.[k] != null)
  if (f.key === 'marriage_status_years') return sent(input, f) || input?.maritalStatus != null
  if (f.key === 'bmi') return sent(input, f) || Boolean(input?.weight && input?.height)
  if (f.key === 'fsh_lh') return sent(input, f) || (input?.fsh != null && input?.lh > 0)
  if (f.key === 'waist_hip_ratio') return sent(input, f) || Boolean(input?.waist && input?.hip)
  return sent(input, f)
}

export const countEntered = (input) => (input ? FEATURES.filter((f) => wasEntered(f, input)).length : 0)

const trim = (n) => String(Math.round(n * 100) / 100)

// Turns the value the model actually saw into something readable.
export function describeModelValue(f, v) {
  if (v == null) return ''
  if (f.key === 'cycle') return v >= 3 ? 'Irregular' : 'Regular'
  if (f.kind === 'yesno') return v >= 0.5 ? 'Yes' : 'No'
  if (f.kind === 'select') return BLOOD_GROUPS.find((b) => Number(b.value) === Number(v))?.label ?? trim(v)
  return f.unit && f.unit !== 'count' ? `${trim(v)} ${f.unit}` : trim(v)
}

// Made-up values for trying the form. They are not patient data and say nothing about the model.
export const TEST_VALUES = {
  typical: {
    age: '29', weight: '56', height: '163', pulse_rate: '74', respiratory_rate: '18', hb: '12.4',
    bp_systolic: '112', bp_diastolic: '74', waist: '28', hip: '36',

    bp_systolic: '120', bp_diastolic: '80', waist: '33', hip: '38',
    cycle: 'irregular', cycle_length: '5', marriage_status_years: '2', pregnant: 'no', abortions: '0',
```

### FILE: frontend/src/lib/format.js  (20 lines)

```
import { BANDS } from '../config'

export const bandOf = (p) => BANDS.find((b) => p < b.max) ?? BANDS[BANDS.length - 1]

export const pct = (p) => `${Number((p * 100).toFixed(2))}%`

// The server sometimes sends times without a timezone (MongoDB returns naive UTC values).
// Treat those as UTC so every date is shown in the viewer's own time zone.
export function parseDate(value) {
  if (!value) return null
  const s = String(value)
  const d = new Date(/(Z|[+-]\d\d:?\d\d)$/.test(s) ? s : `${s}Z`)
  return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d
}

export function fmtDate(value) {
  const d = parseDate(value)
  if (!d) return ''
  return d.toLocaleString(undefined, { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric', hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' })
}
```

### FILE: frontend/src/lib/results.js  (88 lines)
```
// Turns the backend's analysis JSON into one shape the screens can rely on.
//
// Backend fields:
//   clinical, ultrasound   { prediction, probability, model_version, error? }
//   combined               { prediction, probability, confidence_score, method, model_version }
//   explainability         { clinical: { method, status, feature_importance: [{ feature, importance, value }] }, ... }
//
// A model result counts as usable only when it has a probability and a real PCOS/Normal label.
// Fallback labels such as UNAVAILABLE, NOT_AVAILABLE, NOT_PROVIDED and ERROR are shown as notes.

import { featureForColumn } from './features'

const LABELS = new Set(['PCOS', 'Normal'])

function modality(m) {
  if (!m) return { usable: false, probability: null, label: 'NOT_AVAILABLE', note: 'Not available' }
  const p = typeof m.probability === 'number' ? m.probability : null
  const usable = p !== null && LABELS.has(m.prediction)
  let note = ''
  if (!usable) {
    if (m.message) note = m.message
    else if (m.prediction === 'NOT_AVAILABLE' || m.prediction === 'NOT_PROVIDED') note = 'Not available'
    else if (m.prediction === 'ERROR') note = m.error ? `The model failed: ${m.error}` : 'The model failed on this input.'
    else note = 'The model was not available.'
  }
  return { usable, probability: p, label: m.prediction, version: m.model_version, note }
}

function readImportance(clinicalExplain) {
  const list = clinicalExplain?.top_features ?? clinicalExplain?.feature_importance
  if (!Array.isArray(list)) return []
  return list
    .filter((r) => r && (typeof r.shap === 'number' || typeof r.importance === 'number'))
    .map((r) => ({
      feature: featureForColumn(r.feature) ?? null,
      name: r.feature,
      importance: typeof r.shap === 'number' ? Math.abs(r.shap) : r.importance,
      shap: typeof r.shap === 'number' ? r.shap : undefined,
      direction: r.direction,
      value: r.value,
    }))
}

// build_explanation() adds some summary text. Its key names are not documented, so any plain string
// directly under `explainability` is shown as a paragraph.
function readSummary(explain) {
  if (!explain || typeof explain !== 'object') return []
  return Object.entries(explain)
    .filter(([k, v]) => k !== 'clinical' && k !== 'ultrasound' && typeof v === 'string' && v.trim())
    .map(([, v]) => v.trim())
}

export function toReport(raw) {
  const explain = raw.explainability ?? {}
  const c = raw.combined ?? {}
  return {
    id: raw.analysis_id,
    createdAt: raw.created_at,
    patientId: raw.patient_id,
    patientName: raw.patient_name,
    clinicalInput: raw.clinical_input ?? null,
    clinical: modality(raw.clinical ?? raw.clinical_report),
    ultrasound: modality(raw.ultrasound ?? raw.ultrasound_report),
    combined: {
      probability: typeof c.probability === 'number' ? c.probability : null,
      label: c.prediction,
      confidence: typeof c.confidence_score === 'number' ? c.confidence_score : null,
      method: c.method ?? 'insufficient_data',
      version: c.model_version,
    },
    importance: readImportance(explain.clinical),
    importanceStatus: explain.clinical?.status,
    importanceMethod: explain.clinical?.method,
    heatmapUrl: explain.ultrasound?.heatmap_overlay_png_base64
      ? `data:image/png;base64,${explain.ultrasound.heatmap_overlay_png_base64}`
      : null,
    summary: readSummary(explain),
    hasExplanation: Boolean(raw.explainability),
  }
}

export const METHOD_TEXT = {
  dual_modal_weighted_average: 'Equal-weight average of the clinical and ultrasound probabilities.',
  clinical_only: 'Based on the clinical values provided.',
  ultrasound_only: 'Based on the ultrasound image.',
  legacy: 'Saved result from an earlier analysis version.',
  insufficient_data: 'There was not enough data to produce a combined result.',
}
```

### FILE: frontend/src/lib/router.jsx  (66 lines)
```
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'

// A tiny hash router: links look like #/about. It needs no server setup, so the built site
// works from any static host or even a plain folder.
//
// Routes are listed in ROUTES. A route ending in "/:id" matches any single segment after it and
// passes it on as params.id, for example #/reports/12661ac5-... matches '/reports/:id'.

export const ROUTES = {
  '/': 'Home',
  '/screen': 'Screen',
  '/reports': 'My reports',
  '/reports/:id': 'Report',
  '/sign-in': 'Sign in',
  '/about': 'About PCOS',
  '/how-it-works': 'How it works',
}

const patterns = Object.keys(ROUTES).map((route) => ({
  route,
  parts: route.split('/').filter(Boolean),
}))

function match(path) {
  const segs = path.split('/').filter(Boolean)
  for (const { route, parts } of patterns) {
    if (parts.length !== segs.length) continue
    const params = {}
    const ok = parts.every((p, i) => {
      if (p.startsWith(':')) {
        params[p.slice(1)] = decodeURIComponent(segs[i])
        return true
      }
      return p === segs[i]
    })
    if (ok) return { route, params }
  }
  return { route: '/', params: {} }
}

const read = () => match(window.location.hash.replace(/^#/, '').split('?')[0] || '/')

export function useRoute() {
  const [state, setState] = useState(read)
  useEffect(() => {
    const onChange = () => {
      setState(read())
      window.scrollTo(0, 0)
    }
    window.addEventListener('hashchange', onChange)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('hashchange', onChange)
  }, [])
  return state
}

export const navigate = (to) => {
  window.location.hash = to
}

export function Link({ to, children, ...rest }) {
  return (
    <a href={`#${to}`} {...rest}>
      {children}
    </a>
  )
}
```

### FILE: frontend/src/lib/synthetic.js  (143 lines)
```
// Draws synthetic, ultrasound-looking images (speckle, a fan-shaped field, one ovary with dark
// follicles). They are not real scans. They power the built-in examples, the illustrations
// and the hero banners.
//
// peripheral   many small follicles near the edge of the ovary and a brighter centre, which is
//              how polycystic ovarian morphology is usually drawn
// width/height drawing size before upscaling; the fan is sized from the height
// apexX        where the top of the fan sits across the width (0 to 1)
// transparent  leave everything outside the fan see-through, so it can sit on a white page
// tint         'gray' or 'teal'
// scale        upscaling factor for the returned canvas
// glow         soft pink and amber glow over some follicles (needs a see-through or opaque fan)

function mulberry32(a) {
  return function () {
    a |= 0
    a = (a + 0x6d2b79f5) | 0
    let t = Math.imul(a ^ (a >>> 15), 1 | a)
    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296
  }
}

const smooth = (a, b, x) => {
  const t = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (x - a) / (b - a)))
  return t * t * (3 - 2 * t)
}

const TINTS = { gray: [1, 1, 1], teal: [0.42, 0.86, 0.9] }

export function renderSonogram({
  follicles = 10,
  seed = 1,
  peripheral = false,
  width = 320,
  height = 240,
  apexX = 0.5,
  transparent = false,
  tint = 'gray',
  scale = 2,
  glow = false,
} = {}) {
  const W = width
  const H = height
  const s = H / 240
  const rnd = mulberry32(seed)
  const apex = { x: W * apexX, y: 6 * s }
  const R = 225 * s
  const half = 0.66
  const ov = { x: apex.x + 6 * s, y: 118 * s, rx: 82 * s, ry: 50 * s, rot: -0.12 }
  const stromaBoost = peripheral ? 38 : 26
  const [tr, tg, tb] = TINTS[tint] ?? TINTS.gray

  const fol = []
  let guard = 0
  while (fol.length < follicles && guard++ < 6000) {
    const a = rnd() * Math.PI * 2
    const d = peripheral ? 0.6 + rnd() * 0.28 : Math.sqrt(rnd())
    const lx = Math.cos(a) * d * ov.rx * (peripheral ? 0.9 : 0.86)
    const ly = Math.sin(a) * d * ov.ry * (peripheral ? 0.9 : 0.8)
    const x = ov.x + lx * Math.cos(ov.rot) - ly * Math.sin(ov.rot)
    const y = ov.y + lx * Math.sin(ov.rot) + ly * Math.cos(ov.rot)
    const r = (peripheral ? 3.2 + rnd() * 2.8 : 4 + rnd() * 4.5) * s
    const gap = (peripheral ? 1.2 : 2) * s
    if (fol.every((f) => Math.hypot(f.x - x, f.y - y) > f.r + r + gap)) fol.push({ x, y, r })
  }

  const small = document.createElement('canvas')
  small.width = W
  small.height = H
  const g = small.getContext('2d')
  const img = g.createImageData(W, H)
  const px = img.data

  for (let y = 0; y < H; y++) {
    for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) {
      const i = (y * W + x) * 4
      const dx = x - apex.x
      const dy = y - apex.y
      const r = Math.hypot(dx, dy)
      const th = Math.atan2(dx, dy)
      let v = 0
      let a = transparent ? 0 : 255
      if (dy > 0 && Math.abs(th) < half && r < R) {
        const edge = smooth(0, 6 * s, (half - Math.abs(th)) * r) * smooth(0, 6 * s, R - r)
        const speckle = (rnd() + rnd() + rnd()) / 3
        let base = 58 + speckle * 70 - (r * 0.12) / s
        const ex = x - ov.x
        const ey = y - ov.y
        const lx = ex * Math.cos(-ov.rot) - ey * Math.sin(-ov.rot)
        const ly = ex * Math.sin(-ov.rot) + ey * Math.cos(-ov.rot)
        const q = Math.hypot(lx / ov.rx, ly / ov.ry)
        base += (1 - smooth(0.85, 1.0, q)) * stromaBoost
        base += smooth(0.9, 1.0, q) * (1 - smooth(1.0, 1.1, q)) * 40
        for (const f of fol) {
          const dist = Math.hypot(x - f.x, y - f.y)
          base *= 1 - 0.92 * (1 - smooth(f.r * 0.8, f.r * 1.1, dist))
        }
        base = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, base))
        if (transparent) {
          v = base
          a = Math.round(edge * 255)
        } else {
          v = base * edge
        }
      }
      px[i] = v * tr
      px[i + 1] = v * tg
      px[i + 2] = v * tb
      px[i + 3] = a
    }
  }
  g.putImageData(img, 0, 0)

  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')
  canvas.width = W * scale
  canvas.height = H * scale
  const og = canvas.getContext('2d')
  og.imageSmoothingEnabled = true
  og.imageSmoothingQuality = 'high'
  og.drawImage(small, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height)

  // Attention blobs in 0..1 image coordinates, one per follicle.
  const regions = fol.map((f) => ({ x: f.x / W, y: f.y / H, r: (f.r * 2.2) / W, w: 0.9 }))

  if (glow) {
    og.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-atop'
    for (const f of fol.slice(0, 7)) {
      const cx = f.x * scale
      const cy = f.y * scale
      const rad = f.r * 2.4 * scale
      const grad = og.createRadialGradient(cx, cy, 0, cx, cy, rad)
      grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,96,140,0.75)')
      grad.addColorStop(0.5, 'rgba(255,176,96,0.3)')
      grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,176,96,0)')
      og.fillStyle = grad
      og.fillRect(cx - rad, cy - rad, rad * 2, rad * 2)
    }
    og.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over'
  }

  return { canvas, regions }
}
```

```
import Container from '../components/Container'
import PageHero from '../components/PageHero'
import ScanImage from '../components/visuals/ScanImage'
import OvaryDiagram from '../components/visuals/OvaryDiagram'
import { CycleIcon, HormoneIcon, ScanIcon } from '../components/visuals/Icons'
import { textLink } from '../components/buttons'

const WHO = 'https://www.who.int/news-room/fact-sheets/detail/polycystic-ovary-syndrome'
const GUIDELINE = 'https://www.mja.com.au/journal/2024/221/7/summary-2023-international-evidence-based-guideline-assessment-and-management'

function External({ href, children }) {
  return (
    <a href={href} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer" className={textLink}>
      {children}
      <span className="sr-only"> (opens in a new tab)</span>
    </a>
  )
}

const CRITERIA = [
  {
    Icon: CycleIcon,
    title: 'Irregular or absent periods',
    text: 'Cycles that are infrequent, unpredictable or missing, which points to ovulation not happening regularly.',
  },
  {
    Icon: HormoneIcon,
    title: 'Signs of high androgens',
    text: 'Excess facial or body hair, acne, oily skin or hair loss, or a raised testosterone level in a blood test.',
  },
  {
    Icon: ScanIcon,
    title: 'Polycystic ovaries',
    text: 'Seen on an ultrasound. In adults, a raised anti-Müllerian hormone (AMH) level can stand in for the ultrasound.',
  },
]

export default function About() {
  return (
    <>
      <PageHero page="about" tag="Overview • Diagnosis • Ultrasound" title="What PCOS is and how it is diagnosed">
        Polycystic ovary syndrome (PCOS) is a hormonal condition that affects the ovaries and the menstrual cycle. This
        page covers the basics the screening tool is built around.
      </PageHero>

      <section className="border-t border-line bg-paper py-20">
        <Container className="grid gap-12 lg:grid-cols-2 lg:gap-20">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-3xl font-semibold">How common it is</h2>
            <p className="mt-4 max-w-[56ch] text-lg text-ink-2">
              The World Health Organization estimates that 10 to 13 percent of women of reproductive age have PCOS, and that up
              to 70 percent of them are undiagnosed. <External href={WHO}>WHO fact sheet</External>
            </p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-3xl font-semibold">What it can look like</h2>
            <ul className="mt-4 max-w-[56ch] list-disc space-y-1.5 pl-5 text-lg text-ink-2 marker:text-teal">
              <li>Irregular, infrequent or absent periods</li>
              <li>Excess facial or body hair</li>
              <li>Acne or oily skin</li>
              <li>Hair thinning</li>
              <li>Difficulty conceiving</li>
            </ul>
            <p className="mt-4 max-w-[56ch] text-ink-2">
              Symptoms vary and can change over time. Despite the name, ovarian cysts are not required for a diagnosis.
            </p>
          </div>
        </Container>
      </section>

      <Container className="py-20">
        <h2 className="max-w-2xl text-balance text-3xl font-semibold sm:text-4xl">
          Diagnosis needs at least two of three features
        </h2>
        <p className="mt-4 max-w-[60ch] text-lg text-ink-2">
          A clinician looks for these once other causes have been ruled out. <External href={GUIDELINE}>2023 international guideline</External>
        </p>
        <div className="mt-12 grid gap-10 md:grid-cols-3 md:gap-8">
          {CRITERIA.map(({ Icon, title, text }) => (
            <div key={title}>
              <span className="grid h-14 w-14 place-items-center rounded-full bg-teal-tint text-teal-deep">
                <Icon />
              </span>
              <h3 className="mt-5 text-xl font-semibold">{title}</h3>
              <p className="mt-2 max-w-[40ch] text-ink-2">{text}</p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <p className="mt-12 max-w-[64ch] border-l-4 border-teal pl-4 text-ink-2">
          In this tool, a clinical model reads entered values such as cycle patterns, androgen signs, AMH and reported
          follicle measurements, while an image model analyzes an uploaded scan. Neither replaces a clinician weighing all
          diagnostic criteria.
        </p>
      </Container>

      <section className="bg-sono py-20 text-sono-ink">
        <Container>
          <div className="max-w-2xl">
            <h2 className="text-balance text-3xl font-semibold text-white sm:text-4xl">What the ultrasound shows</h2>
            <p className="mt-4 text-lg text-sono-dim">
              Each pair below is a synthetic scan and a diagram of the same idea. They are illustrations, not patient images.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-12 grid gap-14 md:grid-cols-2 md:gap-10">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-xl font-semibold text-white">Typical ovary</h3>
              <div className="mt-4 [&_figcaption]:hidden">
                <ScanImage slot="typical" follicles={5} seed={23} />
              </div>
              <div className="mx-auto mt-6 max-w-sm rounded-xl bg-paper p-4 text-ink">
                <OvaryDiagram variant="typical" />
              </div>
              <p className="mt-4 max-w-[48ch] text-sono-dim">
                A few follicles of different sizes, and sometimes one that has grown larger than the rest.
              </p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-xl font-semibold text-white">Polycystic morphology</h3>
              <div className="mt-4 [&_figcaption]:hidden">
                <ScanImage slot="pcom" follicles={17} seed={11} peripheral />
              </div>
              <div className="mx-auto mt-6 max-w-sm rounded-xl bg-paper p-4 text-ink">
                <OvaryDiagram variant="pcos" />
              </div>
              <p className="mt-4 max-w-[48ch] text-sono-dim">
                Many small follicles, often lined up near the edge of the ovary around denser tissue in the centre. The ovary
                can also be larger.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </Container>
      </section>

      <Container className="py-20">
        <div className="grid gap-12 lg:grid-cols-2 lg:gap-20">
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-3xl font-semibold">Why combine imaging with clinical data</h2>
            <p className="mt-4 max-w-[56ch] text-lg text-ink-2">
              The scan shows the structure of the ovary. Hormone levels and symptoms show what is happening in the body.
              Clinicians weigh both, because either one alone can mislead. This project explores how a model can do the
              same.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-3xl font-semibold">Sources</h2>
            <ul className="mt-4 space-y-2 text-lg">
              <li>
                <External href={WHO}>World Health Organization: Polycystic ovary syndrome</External>
              </li>
              <li>
                <External href={GUIDELINE}>Summary of the 2023 international evidence-based guideline, Medical Journal of Australia</External>
              </li>
            </ul>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Container>
    </>
  )
}
```

### FILE: frontend/src/pages/Home.jsx  (111 lines)
```
import Container from '../components/Container'
import PageHero from '../components/PageHero'
import { ArrowIcon } from '../components/visuals/Icons'
import ReadoutTracks from '../components/ReadoutTracks'
import FeatureInfluence from '../components/FeatureInfluence'
import ScanImage from '../components/visuals/ScanImage'
import { primaryBtn, secondaryBtn } from '../components/buttons'
import { SAMPLE_IMPORTANCE, SAMPLE_ROWS } from '../content/sample'
import { Link } from '../lib/router'

export default function Home() {
  return (
    <>
      <PageHero
        page="home"
        size="tall"
        tag="Ultrasound • Clinical data • Research estimate"
        title="A PCOS screening from two kinds of evidence"
        actions={
          <>
            <Link to="/screen" className={primaryBtn}>
              Start a screening
              <ArrowIcon />
            </Link>
            <Link to="/how-it-works" className={secondaryBtn}>
              How it works
            </Link>
          </>
        }
      >
        This research prototype analyzes an ultrasound image and clinical markers separately, then averages the available
        results into one estimate.
      </PageHero>

      <section className="border-t border-line bg-paper py-20">
        <Container>
          <h2 className="max-w-2xl text-balance text-3xl font-semibold sm:text-4xl">Two models, one estimate</h2>
          <div className="mt-12 grid gap-14 lg:grid-cols-3 lg:gap-10">
            <div>
              <div className="lg:h-[19.5rem]">
                <ScanImage slot="example" follicles={12} seed={3} peripheral caption={false} />
              </div>
              <h3 className="mt-6 text-xl font-semibold">It reads the scan</h3>
              <p className="mt-2 max-w-[44ch] text-ink-2">
                EfficientNet-B0 with gated attention estimates a probability from the image.
              </p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="lg:h-[19.5rem]">
                <FeatureInfluence rows={SAMPLE_IMPORTANCE} heading={false} limit={5} />
              </div>
              <h3 className="mt-6 text-xl font-semibold">It reads clinical markers</h3>
              <p className="mt-2 max-w-[44ch] text-ink-2">
                A Random Forest uses the clinical values you enter. All required values must be present for an estimate.
              </p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <div className="lg:h-[19.5rem]">
                <ReadoutTracks rows={SAMPLE_ROWS} compact className="mt-0" />
              </div>
              <h3 className="mt-6 text-xl font-semibold">It combines the results</h3>
              <p className="mt-2 max-w-[44ch] text-ink-2">
                When both results are available, their probabilities are averaged with equal weight.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <p className="mt-10 text-sm text-ink-3">Every visual in this section is an example, not output from a trained model.</p>
        </Container>
      </section>

      <section className="bg-sono py-20 text-sono-ink">
        <Container>
          <div className="max-w-2xl">
            <h2 className="text-balance text-3xl font-semibold text-white sm:text-4xl">
              What polycystic ovaries can look like on ultrasound
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-4 text-lg text-sono-dim">
              Polycystic ovaries tend to show many small follicles, often along the edge of the ovary. A typical ovary has
              fewer, at different sizes.
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-10 grid gap-8 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <div className="[&_figcaption]:text-sono-dim">
              <ScanImage slot="typical" follicles={5} seed={23} />
            </div>
            <div className="[&_figcaption]:text-sono-dim">
              <ScanImage slot="pcom" follicles={17} seed={11} peripheral />
            </div>
          </div>
          <p className="mt-8">
            <a href="#/about" className="font-semibold text-beam underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-white">
              Learn how PCOS is diagnosed
            </a>
          </p>
        </Container>
      </section>

      <Container className="py-20">
        <div className="max-w-2xl">
          <h2 className="text-balance text-3xl font-semibold sm:text-4xl">Ready to run a screening?</h2>
          <p className="mt-4 text-lg text-ink-2">
            Create an account, add a scan, enter clinical values and keep the result as a PDF report.
          </p>
          <Link to="/screen" className={`${primaryBtn} mt-8`}>
            Open the screening tool
          </Link>
        </div>
      </Container>
    </>
  )
}
```

### FILE: frontend/src/pages/How.jsx  (92 lines)
```
import Container from '../components/Container'
import PageHero from '../components/PageHero'
import FeatureInfluence from '../components/FeatureInfluence'
import ReadoutTracks from '../components/ReadoutTracks'
import Pipeline from '../components/visuals/Pipeline'
import ScanImage from '../components/visuals/ScanImage'
import { primaryBtn } from '../components/buttons'
import { SAMPLE_IMPORTANCE, SAMPLE_ROWS } from '../content/sample'
import { Link } from '../lib/router'

const PARTS = [
  {
    title: 'The ultrasound model',
    text: 'EfficientNet-B0 extracts image features and a gated-attention layer pools them before the classifier. The image model returns a two-class probability.',
  },
  {
    title: 'The clinical model',
    text: 'A Random Forest reads 41 clinical features, including follicle and endometrium measurements. All required form values must be provided; BMI and two ratios are calculated from their source values.',
  },
  { title: 'The combination', text: 'When both model probabilities are available, the server averages them equally. If only one is available, that result is used by itself.' },
]

const LIMITS = [
  'The 35 and 65 percent band edges are display settings in config.js. They have not been clinically validated.',
  'The clinical model returns no estimate when required values are missing. An ultrasound estimate requires an uploaded scan.',
  'SHAP values describe how clinical features affect this model result; Grad-CAM++ highlights image regions used by the image model. Neither establishes medical causation.',
  'Outputs are estimates for research. They are not a diagnosis.',
]

export default function How() {
  return (
    <>
      <PageHero page="how" tag="Model • Inputs • Limits" title="How the screening works">
        Ultrasound images and clinical markers are processed by separate models. When both results are available, they are
        averaged equally into one estimate.
      </PageHero>

      <section className="border-t border-line bg-paper py-20">
        <Container>
          <h2 className="text-3xl font-semibold">The pipeline</h2>
          <div className="mt-12">
            <Pipeline />
          </div>
        </Container>
      </section>

      <Container className="py-20">
        <div className="grid gap-12 md:grid-cols-3 md:gap-10">
          {PARTS.map((p) => (
            <div key={p.title}>
              <h3 className="text-xl font-semibold">{p.title}</h3>
              <p className="mt-2 max-w-[44ch] text-ink-2">{p.text}</p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </Container>

      <section className="bg-sono py-20 text-sono-ink">
        <Container>
          <h2 className="text-3xl font-semibold text-white">What you get back</h2>
          <p className="mt-3 max-w-[58ch] text-lg text-sono-dim">An example result, with the same pieces the screening page shows.</p>
          <div className="mt-10 grid items-center gap-10 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_1fr] lg:gap-16">
            <div className="[&_figcaption]:text-sono-dim">
              <ScanImage slot="example" follicles={12} seed={3} peripheral />
            </div>
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-paper p-6 text-ink sm:p-8">
              <ReadoutTracks rows={SAMPLE_ROWS} compact className="mt-0" />
              <div className="mt-8 border-t border-line pt-6">
                <FeatureInfluence rows={SAMPLE_IMPORTANCE} heading={false} limit={5} />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <p className="mt-8 text-sm text-sono-dim">These numbers are illustrative. They are not output from a trained model.</p>
        </Container>
      </section>

      <Container className="py-20">
        <div className="grid gap-12 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_1.2fr] lg:gap-20">
          <h2 className="text-3xl font-semibold">Limits to keep in mind</h2>
          <ul className="max-w-[62ch] list-disc space-y-3 pl-5 text-lg text-ink-2 marker:text-teal">
            {LIMITS.map((l) => (
              <li key={l}>{l}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
        <Link to="/screen" className={`${primaryBtn} mt-12`}>
          Start a screening
        </Link>
      </Container>
    </>
  )
}
```

```
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import AuthGate from '../components/AuthGate'
import Container from '../components/Container'
import ReportActions from '../components/ReportActions'
import ReportView from '../components/ReportView'
import { getAnalysis } from '../lib/api'
import { countEntered } from '../lib/features'
import { toReport } from '../lib/results'
import { Link } from '../lib/router'

function Detail({ id }) {
  const [state, setState] = useState({ status: 'loading' })

  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true
    setState({ status: 'loading' })
    getAnalysis(id)
      .then((raw) => alive && setState({ status: 'ok', report: toReport(raw) }))
      .catch((e) => alive && setState({ status: 'error', message: e.message }))
    return () => {
      alive = false
    }
  }, [id])

  return (
    <Container className="pb-24 pt-10">
      <Link to="/reports" className="font-semibold text-teal-deep underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-ink">
        ← All results
      </Link>

      {state.status === 'loading' && <p className="mt-10 text-ink-3">Loading the result</p>}
      {state.status === 'error' && (
        <p role="alert" className="mt-10 text-lg font-semibold text-berry">
          {state.message}
        </p>
      )}
      {state.status === 'ok' && (
        <>
          <h1 className="mt-6 text-3xl font-semibold sm:text-4xl">
            Screening result{state.report.patientName ? ` for ${state.report.patientName}` : ''}
          </h1>
          <div className="mt-10">
            <ReportView report={state.report} entered={countEntered(state.report.clinicalInput)}>
              <ReportActions analysisId={state.report.id} />
            </ReportView>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </Container>
  )
}

export default function ReportDetail({ params }) {
  return (
    <AuthGate next={`/reports/${params.id}`} title="Sign in to see this result" text="Saved results are only shown to the account that made them, or to a doctor.">
      <Detail id={params.id} />
    </AuthGate>
  )
}
```

### FILE: frontend/src/pages/Reports.jsx  (265 lines)
```
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import AnalysisList from '../components/AnalysisList'
import AuthGate from '../components/AuthGate'
import Container from '../components/Container'
import ReportDownload from '../components/ReportDownload'
import TextField from '../components/TextField'
import { primaryBtn } from '../components/buttons'
import { createAccessCode, getDoctorHistory, getHistory, listReports, redeemAccessCode } from '../lib/api'
import { useAuth } from '../lib/auth'
import { fmtDate } from '../lib/format'
import { Link } from '../lib/router'

const smallBtn =
  'rounded-lg border border-line bg-white px-5 py-2.5 font-semibold text-ink shadow-sm transition-colors hover:border-ink-3 disabled:opacity-60'

function Section({ id, title, text, children }) {
  return (
    <section aria-labelledby={id} className="mt-14 first:mt-0">
      <h2 id={id} className="text-2xl font-semibold">
        {title}
      </h2>
      {text && <p className="mt-1 max-w-[62ch] text-ink-2">{text}</p>}
      {children}
    </section>
  )
}

// Patients make a short-lived code and read it to their doctor.
function ShareCode() {
  const [code, setCode] = useState(null)
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false)

  const make = async () => {
    setBusy(true)
    setError('')
    setCopied(false)
    try {
      setCode(await createAccessCode())
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e.message)
    } finally {
      setBusy(false)
    }
  }

  const copy = async () => {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(code.access_code)
      setCopied(true)
    } catch {
      setCopied(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <Section
      id="share-h"
      title="Share with a doctor"
      text="Make a one-time access code and give it to your doctor. It works for 30 minutes and can be used once."
    >
      <button type="button" onClick={make} disabled={busy} className={`${smallBtn} mt-4`}>
        {busy ? 'Creating' : code ? 'Make a new code' : 'Make an access code'}
      </button>
      {code && (
        <div className="mt-5 flex w-fit max-w-full flex-wrap items-center gap-x-5 gap-y-2 rounded-xl border border-line bg-paper px-5 py-4">
          <span className="font-display text-3xl font-semibold tracking-widest" aria-label={`Access code ${code.access_code}`}>
            {code.access_code}
          </span>
          <button type="button" onClick={copy} className="font-semibold text-teal-deep underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-ink">
            {copied ? 'Copied' : 'Copy'}
          </button>
          <span className="text-sm text-ink-3">Expires {fmtDate(code.expires_at)}</span>
        </div>
      )}
      {error && (
        <p role="alert" className="mt-3 font-semibold text-berry">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
    </Section>
  )
}

// Doctors type the code a patient gave them.
function RedeemCode({ onLinked }) {
  const [code, setCode] = useState('')
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [done, setDone] = useState('')

  const submit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!code.trim()) return setError('Enter the code your patient gave you.')
    setBusy(true)
    setError('')
    setDone('')
    try {
      const res = await redeemAccessCode(code)
      onLinked(res.patient_id)
      setDone(res.message || 'Patient access granted')
      setCode('')
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message)
    } finally {
      setBusy(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <Section id="redeem-h" title="Patient access code" text="Enter the one-time code a patient made on their account.">
      <form onSubmit={submit} className="mt-4 flex max-w-lg flex-wrap items-end gap-3" noValidate>
        <TextField
          label="Access code"
          value={code}
          onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
          placeholder="PCOS-XXXXXXXX"
          autoComplete="off"
          spellCheck={false}
          className="min-w-[12rem] flex-1"
        />
        <button type="submit" disabled={busy} className="rounded-lg bg-teal px-6 py-3 font-semibold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-teal-deep disabled:opacity-60">
          {busy ? 'Checking' : 'Use code'}
        </button>
      </form>
      {done && (
        <p role="status" className="mt-3 font-semibold text-teal-deep">
          {done}. Their results are marked in the list below.
        </p>
      )}
      {error && (
        <p role="alert" className="mt-3 font-semibold text-berry">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
    </Section>
  )
}

function useLoad(load) {
  const [state, setState] = useState({ status: 'loading', data: [] })
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true
    load()
      .then((data) => alive && setState({ status: 'ok', data }))
      .catch((e) => alive && setState({ status: 'error', data: [], message: e.message }))
    return () => {
      alive = false
    }
  }, [load])
  return state
}

function Results({ isDoctor, linked }) {
  const state = useLoad(isDoctor ? getDoctorHistory : getHistory)
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('')
  const items = isDoctor
    ? state.data.filter((r) => (r.patient_name || '').toLowerCase().includes(query.trim().toLowerCase()))
    : state.data

  return (
    <Section
      id="results-h"
      title={isDoctor ? 'Saved results' : 'Your screenings'}
      text={isDoctor ? 'Every saved screening, newest first.' : 'Every screening you saved, newest first.'}
    >
      {isDoctor && (
        <TextField
          label="Search by patient name"
          value={query}
          onChange={(e) => setQuery(e.target.value)}
          className="mt-4 max-w-sm"
          autoComplete="off"
        />
      )}
      {state.status === 'loading' && <p className="mt-4 text-ink-3">Loading</p>}
      {state.status === 'error' && (
        <p role="alert" className="mt-4 font-semibold text-berry">
          {state.message}
        </p>
      )}
      {state.status === 'ok' && (
        <AnalysisList
          items={items}
          showPatient={isDoctor}
          linked={linked}
          empty={isDoctor && query ? 'No results match that name.' : isDoctor ? 'No screenings have been saved yet.' : 'You have not saved a screening yet.'}
        />
      )}
      {!isDoctor && state.status === 'ok' && (
        <Link to="/screen" className={`${primaryBtn} mt-6`}>
          Start a screening
        </Link>
      )}
    </Section>
  )
}

function Pdfs({ isDoctor }) {
  const state = useLoad(listReports)
  return (
    <Section id="pdf-h" title="PDF reports" text="Reports you made from a result. Downloading one needs a code that is emailed to your account.">
      {state.status === 'loading' && <p className="mt-4 text-ink-3">Loading</p>}
      {state.status === 'error' && (
        <p role="alert" className="mt-4 font-semibold text-berry">
          {state.message}
        </p>
      )}
      {state.status === 'ok' &&
        (state.data.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="mt-4 text-ink-3">No reports yet. Open a result and choose Create PDF report.</p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="mt-4 divide-y divide-line overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-line bg-white">
            {state.data.map((r) => (
              <li key={r.report_id} className="flex flex-wrap items-start gap-x-8 gap-y-2 px-5 py-4">
                <div className="min-w-[12rem] flex-1">
                  {isDoctor && <p className="font-semibold">{r.patient_name}</p>}
                  <p className={isDoctor ? 'text-sm text-ink-3' : 'font-semibold'}>{fmtDate(r.created_at)}</p>
                </div>
                <Link to={`/reports/${r.analysis_id}`} className="font-semibold text-teal-deep underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-ink">
                  View result
                </Link>
                <ReportDownload reportId={r.report_id} />
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        ))}
    </Section>
  )
}

function Dashboard() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const isDoctor = user.role === 'doctor'
  const [linked, setLinked] = useState(() => new Set())

  return (
    <>
      <div className="border-b border-line bg-paper">
        <Container className="py-12 sm:py-14">
          <h1 className="text-4xl font-semibold text-teal">{isDoctor ? 'Patients' : 'My reports'}</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 max-w-[60ch] text-lg text-ink-2">
            {isDoctor
              ? 'Review saved screenings and use access codes from your patients.'
              : 'Your saved screenings and PDF reports, and a way to share them with a doctor.'}
          </p>
        </Container>
      </div>
      <Container className="py-12 pb-24">
        {isDoctor ? <RedeemCode onLinked={(id) => setLinked((s) => new Set(s).add(id))} /> : <ShareCode />}
        <Results isDoctor={isDoctor} linked={linked} />
        <Pdfs isDoctor={isDoctor} />
      </Container>
    </>
  )
}

export default function Reports() {
  return (
    <AuthGate next="/reports" title="Sign in to see your reports" text="Your saved screenings and reports are kept on your account.">
      <Dashboard />
    </AuthGate>
  )
}
```

### FILE: frontend/src/pages/Screen.jsx  (169 lines)
```
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import AuthGate from '../components/AuthGate'
import ClinicalForm from '../components/ClinicalForm'
import Container from '../components/Container'
import PageHero from '../components/PageHero'
import ReportActions from '../components/ReportActions'
import ReportView from '../components/ReportView'
import RunBar from '../components/RunBar'
import { APP, IMAGE_EXTENSIONS, MAX_UPLOAD_MB } from '../config'
import { runAnalysis } from '../lib/api'
import { buildPayload, countProvided, emptyClinical, FEATURES, parseClinical } from '../lib/features'
import { toReport } from '../lib/results'

function ScreenTool() {
  const [image, setImage] = useState(null)
  const [imageUrl, setImageUrl] = useState(null)
  const [imageError, setImageError] = useState('')
  const [clinical, setClinical] = useState(emptyClinical)
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('idle') // idle | running | done | error
  const [result, setResult] = useState(null) // { report, entered }
  const [stale, setStale] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const resultsRef = useRef(null)

  const provided = useMemo(() => countProvided(clinical), [clinical])
  const hasErrors = useMemo(() => Object.keys(parseClinical(clinical).errors).length > 0, [clinical])
  const running = status === 'running'
  const canRun = (Boolean(image) || provided > 0) && !hasErrors && !imageError && !running

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!image) {
      setImageUrl(null)
      return undefined
    }
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(image)
    setImageUrl(url)
    return () => URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
  }, [image])

  const inputsChanged = () => {
    if (result) setStale(true)
  }

  const changeField = (key, value) => {
    setClinical((c) => ({ ...c, [key]: value }))
    inputsChanged()
  }

  const changeImage = (file) => {
    if (!file) return
    const extension = `.${file.name.split('.').pop().toLowerCase()}`
    if (!IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.includes(extension)) {
      setImageError('Choose a JPG, PNG or WebP image.')
      return
    }
    if (file.size > MAX_UPLOAD_MB * 1024 * 1024) {
      setImageError(`Choose an image smaller than ${MAX_UPLOAD_MB} MB.`)
      return
    }
    setImageError('')
    setImage(file)
    inputsChanged()
  }

  const clearAll = () => {
    setImage(null)
    setImageError('')
    setClinical(emptyClinical())
    setResult(null)
    setStale(false)
    setError('')
    setStatus('idle')
  }

  const run = async () => {
    setStatus('running')
    setError('')
    setStale(false)
    try {
      const raw = await runAnalysis({ clinical: buildPayload(clinical), image })
      setResult({ report: toReport(raw), entered: provided })
      setStatus('done')
      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        const calm = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches
        resultsRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: calm ? 'auto' : 'smooth', block: 'start' })
      })
    } catch (e) {
      setError(e?.message || 'The analysis failed. Try again.')
      setStatus('error')
    }
  }

  const hint = running
    ? 'Analyzing. This takes a moment.'
    : hasErrors
      ? 'Fix the highlighted values to continue.'
        : imageError
          ? imageError
      : image || provided > 0
        ? `Ready with ${[image && 'an ultrasound image', provided > 0 && `${provided} clinical value${provided === 1 ? '' : 's'}`].filter(Boolean).join(' and ')}.`
        : 'Add an ultrasound image or enter at least one clinical value.'
  const note =
    !running && !hasErrors && (image || provided > 0) && provided < FEATURES.length
      ? `Clinical estimate unavailable until all ${FEATURES.length} clinical values are entered.`
      : ''

  return (
    <Container className="pb-24 pt-14">
      <div className="grid gap-14 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,1.1fr)] lg:gap-16">
        <section aria-labelledby="scan-h" className="lg:sticky lg:top-6 lg:self-start">
          <h2 id="scan-h" className="text-2xl font-semibold">Ultrasound image</h2>
          <p className="mt-1 max-w-[56ch] text-ink-2">{APP.imaging.model} analyzes the scan. You can also run with clinical values only.</p>
          {imageUrl ? (
            <img src={imageUrl} alt="Selected ultrasound scan" className="mt-5 max-h-96 w-full rounded-lg bg-sono object-contain" />
          ) : (
            <label className="mt-5 grid aspect-[4/3] cursor-pointer place-items-center rounded-lg border border-dashed border-line bg-paper px-6 text-center hover:border-teal">
              <span>
                <span className="block font-semibold">Choose an ultrasound image</span>
                <span className="mt-1 block text-sm text-ink-3">JPG, PNG or WebP, up to {MAX_UPLOAD_MB} MB</span>
              </span>
              <input type="file" accept={IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.join(',')} className="sr-only" onChange={(event) => { changeImage(event.target.files?.[0]); event.target.value = '' }} />
            </label>
          )}
          {image && (
            <div className="mt-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-4 text-sm">
              <span className="truncate text-ink-2">{image.name}</span>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => { setImage(null); setImageError(''); inputsChanged() }} className="shrink-0 font-semibold text-teal-deep underline underline-offset-4">Remove image</button>
            </div>
          )}
          {imageError && <p role="alert" className="mt-2 text-sm font-semibold text-berry">{imageError}</p>}
          {image && (
            <label className="mt-3 inline-block cursor-pointer text-sm font-semibold text-teal-deep underline underline-offset-4">
              Replace image
              <input type="file" accept={IMAGE_EXTENSIONS.join(',')} className="sr-only" onChange={(event) => { changeImage(event.target.files?.[0]); event.target.value = '' }} />
            </label>
          )}
        </section>
        <ClinicalForm values={clinical} onChange={changeField} provided={provided} />
      </div>

      <RunBar hint={hint} note={note} canRun={canRun} running={running} error={error} onRun={run} onClear={clearAll} />

      {result && (
        <div className="mt-16 border-t border-line pt-10">
          <ReportView report={result.report} entered={result.entered} imageUrl={imageUrl} stale={stale} resultsRef={resultsRef}>
            {result.report.id && <ReportActions analysisId={result.report.id} />}
          </ReportView>
        </div>
      )}
    </Container>
  )
}

export default function Screen() {
  return (
    <>
      <PageHero page="screen" size="medium" tag="Ultrasound • Clinical values" title="Run a PCOS screening">
        Add an ultrasound image, clinical values, or both. The available model results are combined into a research estimate.
      </PageHero>
      <AuthGate
        next="/screen"
        title="Sign in to run a screening"
        text="Screenings are saved to your account so you can find them again and make a PDF report. Creating an account takes a minute."
      >
        <ScreenTool />
      </AuthGate>
    </>
  )
}
```

```
import { useState } from 'react'
import { getAfterSignIn } from '../components/AuthGate'
import Container from '../components/Container'
import OtpField from '../components/OtpField'
import TextField from '../components/TextField'
import { login, register, verifyLogin, verifyRegistration } from '../lib/api'
import { useAuth } from '../lib/auth'
import { HERO } from '../content/images'
import { Link, navigate } from '../lib/router'

const submitBtn =
  'rounded-lg bg-teal px-8 py-3 text-lg font-semibold text-white shadow-lg shadow-teal/25 transition-colors hover:bg-teal-deep disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60'

const EMAIL = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/

export default function SignIn() {
  const { signIn, status, user } = useAuth()
  const [mode, setMode] = useState('signin') // signin | register
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(null) // { userId, kind: 'login' | 'registration' } once a code was sent
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ full_name: '', email: '', phone: '', password: ***REDACTED***, role: 'patient' })
  const [otp, setOtp] = useState('')
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [info, setInfo] = useState('')
  const [fieldErrors, setFieldErrors] = useState({})

  const set = (k) => (e) => setForm((f) => ({ ...f, [k]: e.target.value }))

  if (status === 'in' && user) {
    return (
      <Container className="py-24">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-semibold">You are signed in</h1>
        <p className="mt-3 text-lg text-ink-2">Signed in as {user.full_name || 'your account'} ({user.role}).</p>
        <Link to="/screen" className="mt-6 inline-block font-semibold text-teal-deep underline underline-offset-4">
          Go to the screening page
        </Link>
      </Container>
    )
  }

  const validate = () => {
    const e = {}
    if (mode === 'register' && form.full_name.trim().length < 2) e.full_name = 'Enter your full name.'
    if (!EMAIL.test(form.email.trim())) e.email = 'Enter a valid email address.'
    if (mode === 'register' ? form.password.length < 8 : !form.password) e.password = ***REDACTED*** === 'register' ? 'Use at least 8 characters.' : 'Enter your password.'
    setFieldErrors(e)
    return Object.keys(e).length === 0
  }

  const sendCode = async (e) => {
    e?.preventDefault()
    if (!validate()) return
    setBusy(true)
    setError('')
    setInfo('')
    try {
      if (mode === 'register') {
        const res = await register({
          email: form.email.trim(),
          password: ***REDACTED***,
          full_name: form.full_name.trim(),
          role: form.role,
          phone: form.phone.trim() || null,
        })
        setPending({ userId: res.user_id, kind: 'registration' })
      } else {
        const res = await login(form.email.trim(), form.password)
        setPending({ userId: res.user_id, kind: res.requires_verification ? 'registration' : 'login' })
      }
      setOtp('')
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message)
    } finally {
      setBusy(false)
    }
  }

  const resend = async () => {
    setBusy(true)
    setError('')
    try {
      // Signing in again sends a fresh code, for new and returning accounts alike.
      const res = await login(form.email.trim(), form.password)
      setPending({ userId: res.user_id, kind: res.requires_verification ? 'registration' : 'login' })
      setInfo('A new code was sent.')
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message)
    } finally {
      setBusy(false)
    }
  }

  const verify = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (otp.length < 6) return setError('Enter the 6-character code.')
    setBusy(true)
    setError('')
    try {
      const data =
        pending.kind === 'registration'
          ? await verifyRegistration(pending.userId, otp)
          : await verifyLogin(pending.userId, otp)
      signIn(data)
      navigate(getAfterSignIn())
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message)
    } finally {
      setBusy(false)
    }
  }

  const switchMode = (m) => {
    setMode(m)
    setError('')
    setFieldErrors({})
  }

  return (
    <Container className="py-14 sm:py-20">
      <div className="grid items-stretch gap-14 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,34rem)_minmax(0,1fr)] lg:gap-20">
        <div>
          {!pending ? (
            <>
              <h1 className="text-4xl font-semibold">{mode === 'signin' ? 'Sign in' : 'Create your account'}</h1>
              <p className="mt-3 max-w-[48ch] text-lg text-ink-2">
                {mode === 'signin'
                  ? 'Your screenings and reports are saved to your account. We email you a code each time you sign in.'
                  : 'An account keeps your screenings and lets you make PDF reports. We email you a code to confirm the address.'}
              </p>

              <div className="mt-8 inline-flex overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-line" role="group" aria-label="Sign in or create an account">
                {[
                  ['signin', 'Sign in'],
                  ['register', 'Create account'],
                ].map(([m, label]) => (
                  <button
                    key={m}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => switchMode(m)}
                    aria-pressed={mode === m}
                    className={`px-5 py-2 font-semibold transition-colors ${
                      mode === m ? 'bg-ink text-paper' : 'bg-white text-ink-2 hover:bg-paper'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {label}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>

              <form onSubmit={sendCode} noValidate className="mt-6 space-y-5">
                {mode === 'register' && (
                  <TextField label="Full name" value={form.full_name} onChange={set('full_name')} autoComplete="name" error={fieldErrors.full_name} />
                )}
                <TextField label="Email" type="email" value={form.email} onChange={set('email')} autoComplete="email" error={fieldErrors.email} />
                {mode === 'register' && (
                  <TextField
                    label="Phone (optional)"
                    type="tel"
                    value={form.phone}
                    onChange={set('phone')}
                    autoComplete="tel"
                  />
                )}
                <TextField
                  label="Password"
                  type="password"
                  value={form.password}
                  onChange={set('password')}
                  autoComplete={mode === 'register' ? 'new-password' : ***REDACTED***}
                  hint={mode === 'register' ? 'At least 8 characters.' : undefined}
                  error={fieldErrors.password}
                />
                {mode === 'register' && (
                  <fieldset>
                    <legend className="text-sm font-semibold text-ink-2">I am a</legend>
                    <div className="mt-2 flex gap-3">
                      {[
                        ['patient', 'Patient', 'Run screenings and keep your own reports.'],
                        ['doctor', 'Doctor', 'Review the screenings of patients who share a code.'],
                      ].map(([value, label, text]) => (
                        <label
                          key={value}
                          className={`flex-1 cursor-pointer rounded-lg border p-3 transition-colors has-[:focus-visible]:outline-3 has-[:focus-visible]:outline-teal ${
                            form.role === value ? 'border-teal bg-teal-tint/40' : 'border-line bg-white hover:border-ink-3'
                          }`}
                        >
                          <input
                            type="radio"
                            name="role"
                            value={value}
                            checked={form.role === value}
                            onChange={set('role')}
                            className="sr-only"
                          />
                          <span className="block font-semibold">{label}</span>
                          <span className="mt-0.5 block text-sm text-ink-2">{text}</span>
                        </label>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  </fieldset>
                )}

                {error && (
                  <p role="alert" className="font-semibold text-berry">
                    {error}
                  </p>
                )}
                <button type="submit" disabled={busy} className={submitBtn}>
                  {busy ? 'Please wait' : mode === 'signin' ? 'Send me a code' : 'Create account'}
                </button>
              </form>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <h1 className="text-4xl font-semibold">Check your email</h1>
              <p className="mt-3 max-w-[48ch] text-lg text-ink-2">
                We sent a 6-character code to <span className="font-semibold text-ink">{form.email.trim()}</span>. It expires in a
                few minutes.
              </p>
              <form onSubmit={verify} className="mt-8 space-y-5">
                <OtpField value={otp} onChange={setOtp} autoFocus />
                {info && (
                  <p role="status" className="text-ink-2">
                    {info}
                  </p>
                )}
                {error && (
                  <p role="alert" className="font-semibold text-berry">
                    {error}
                  </p>
                )}
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-6 gap-y-3">
                  <button type="submit" disabled={busy} className={submitBtn}>
                    {busy ? 'Checking' : pending.kind === 'registration' ? 'Verify and continue' : 'Sign in'}
                  </button>
                  <button type="button" onClick={resend} disabled={busy} className="font-semibold text-teal-deep underline underline-offset-4 hover:text-ink">
                    Send a new code
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      setPending(null)
                      setError('')
                      setInfo('')
                    }}
                    className="font-semibold text-ink-2 hover:text-ink"
                  >
                    Back
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
              <p className="mt-8 max-w-[52ch] text-sm text-ink-3">
                Developing locally? If the server has no email settings, it prints the code in its console instead of
                sending it.
              </p>
            </>
          )}
        </div>

        <div
          className="relative hidden min-h-[30rem] overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-paper lg:block"
          aria-hidden="true"
          style={{
            backgroundImage: `url(${HERO.signin.src})`,
            backgroundSize: 'cover',
            backgroundPosition: HERO.signin.position,
          }}
        >
        </div>
      </div>
    </Container>
  )
}
```
